	nyāsāparaparyāyaprapattinirūpaṇaṃ nāma 
saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya taddivyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

	uktānuvādapūrvakaṃ sudarśanasya 
ṣoḍaśabhujatvaprayojanādipraśnaḥ 

nāradaḥ �

ākhyātaṃ bhavatā sarvaṃ bhagavan parameśvara |
saudarśanasya yantrasya mantrasya svasya ca prabho || 37-1 ||

astrāṇāṃ cāpi māhātmyaṃ svarūpaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ tathā |
ārādhanaṃ ca yogaśca svabhāvaśca viśeṣataḥ || 37-2 ||

bhujaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirveti proktaṃ rūpopavarṇane |
tat kadā kena kartavyaṃ kimarthaṃ kīdṛśaṃ vada || 37-3 ||

rājñāmatyāhitādinivāraṇaṃ ṣoḍaśabhujatvasya prayojanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

parairabhibhave prāpte rājñāṃ balasamanvitaiḥ |
nagareṣu pradagdheṣu rājñāṃ vidrāvite bale || 37-4 ||

p. 354) uparuddheṣu bhogeṣu tattadviṣayavāsinām |
pīḍyamāne parabalairitthaṃ rāṣṭre mahāmune || 37-5 ||

sthitāvanupapannāyāṃ rājño'vyucchinnavairiṇaḥ |
kārayet ṣoḍaśabhujaṃ sudarśanamavāritam || 37-6 ||

ṣoḍaśabhujasudarśanavigrahavarṇanam

apāramaparicchedyamavāṅmanasagocaram |
raktavarṇamudārāṅgaṃ pītakauśeyavāsasam || 37-7 ||

vidyutpuñjapratīkāśaiḥ keśairūrdhvamukhairyutam |
piṅgalāghūrṇamānogranetratritayabhīṣaṇam || 37-8 ||

daṣṭādharasphuṭālakṣyadaṃṣṭrāniṣṭhyūtapāvakam |
kvaṇatkiṅkiṇijālena kāñcīdāmnā virājitam || 37-9 ||

nitambalambinopetaṃ jvalatkaukṣeyakeṇa ca |
dadhānaṃ kiṅkiṇīmālāṃ tvalimālānināditām || 37-10 ||

āpādalambinīṃ divyāṃ muktāratnācitāṃ tathā | 
jhaṇajjhaṇitamañjīraśobhamānapadāmbujam || 37-11 ||

hārakeyūratāṭaṅkamukuṭairupaśobhitam |
anyairmaṇimayaiścitrairbhūṣaṇaiḥ samalaṃkṛtam || 37-12 ||

krameṇa cakraparaśukuntadaṇḍāṅkuśāni ca |
śastrāṇi śatavaktrāṇi khaḍgaśaktī ca bibhratam || 37-13 ||

p. 355) karairūrdhvamukhairdīrghairdakṣiṇairaṣṭabhiḥ paraiḥ |
vāmaiḥ śaṅkhadhanuḥpāśahalavajrāṇi ca kramāt || 37-14 ||

gadāmusalaśūlāni bhīmānyetāni bibhratam |
svasamāśritaśatrūtthakrodhena mahatāvṛtam || 37-15 || 

bhugnabhrūkuṭilaṃ vaktraṃ dhārayantaṃ vibhīṣaṇam |
pratyālīḍhaviśeṣeṇa tiṣṭhantaṃ dīptimālinam || 37-16 ||

kālāgnirudrarūpeṇa saṃharantamidaṃ jagat |

yathoktasudarśanadhyānasya niḥsāpatnyasādhanatā

evaṃ sudarśanaṃ dhyāyet kālātmānaṃ mahādyutim || 37-17 ||

rājā taddhyānamātreṇa niḥsapatno bhavet kṣaṇāt | 
na kvāpyupasthitisteṣāṃ sapatnānāṃ mahītale || 37-18 ||

naśyanti śatravastatra yatra yatra ca vidrutāḥ |

sudarśanamanārādhayatāṃ rājñāṃ śatruparābhavādiḥ

evamenamanārādhya rājāno'mitavikramāḥ || 37-19 ||

sapatnairabhibhūyante niḥśrīkāśca bhavanti vai | 

yogena nyāsena vā sudarśanārādhanasyaihikāmuṣmikaphalasādhanatā

ya enaṃ pūjayedbhaktyā pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā || 37-20 ||

bāhyena cāntareṇāpi yogākhyena mahāmune | 
nyāsena vā sukhī so'tra paratra ca na saṃśayaḥ || 37-21 ||

p. 356) uktānuvādapūrvakaṃ nyāsākhyaprapattisvarūpapraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

bāhya ābhyantaraścāpi mama yogaḥ puroditaḥ |
nyāsākhyaṃ sādhanaṃ proktaṃ tanme brūhi maheśvara || 37-22 ||

nyāsasya paramarahasyatvakhyāpanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

etanmahopaniṣadaṃ devānāṃ guhyamuttamam |
abhīṣṭārthapradaṃ sadyaḥ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 37-23 ||

avācyametat sarvasmai nābhaktāya kadācana |
bhakto'si me sthiraśceti vakṣyāmi hitakāmyayā || 37-24 ||

nyāsasya sarvaphalasādhanatvopapādanam

yadyena kāmakāmena nāsādyaṃ sādhanāntaraiḥ |
mumukṣuṇā yat sāṃkhyena yogena na ca bhaktitaḥ || 37-25 ||

prāpyate paramaṃ dhāma yato nāvartate punaḥ |
tena tenāpyate tattannyāsenaiva mahāmune || 37-26 ||

paramātmā ca tenaiva sādhyate puruṣottamaḥ |

prapatteḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni

ṣoḍhā hi vedaviduṣo vadantyenaṃ mahāmune || 37-27 ||

p. 357) ānukūlyasya saṃkalpaḥ prātikūlyasya varjanam |
rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ tathā || 37-28 ||

ātmanikṣepakārpaṇye ṣaḍvidhā śaraṇāgatiḥ |

nyāsaprakaraṇe śaraṇaśabdasya sādhanavācitvam

upāye gṛharakṣitroḥ śabdaḥ śaraṇamityayam || 37-29 ||

vartate sāṃprataṃ caiṣa upāyārthaikavācakaḥ |

prapattilakṣaṇam

ahamasmyaparādhānāmālayo'kiṃcano'gatiḥ || 37-30 ||

tvamevopāyabhūto me bhaveti prārthanāmatiḥ |
śaraṇāgatirityuktā sā deve'smin prayujyatām || 37-31 ||

sudarśanaprapattau mantraviśeṣakathanam

bhagavan sarvavijayisahasrārāparājita | 
śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ prapanno'smi śrīkaraṃ śrīsudarśanam || 37-32 ||

anena mantreṇa prapannasya phalotpattipratibandhakasakaladuritanivṛttiḥ

anenaiva prapannasya bhagavantaṃ sudarśanam |
tasyānubandhāḥ pāpmānaḥ sarve naśyantyasaṃśayam || 37-33 ||

prapannasya kṛtakṛtyatvam

kṛtānyanena sarvāṇi tapāṃsi tapatāṃ vara |
sarve tīrthāḥ sarvayajñāḥ sarvadānāni ca kṣaṇāt || 37-34 ||

p. 358) kṛtānyanena mokṣaśca tasya haste na saṃśayaḥ |

nyāsamātreṇa tapoyajñādisiddhikathaṃtāpraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

kathaṃ tapāṃsi yajñāśca tīrthadānāni sarvaśaḥ || 37-35 ||

tasyaiva nyāsamātreṇa sidhyante tadvada prabho |

taduttarakathane nyāsasya sarvatapo'tiriktatapastvakathanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

yāni niḥśreyasārthāni coditāni tapāṃsi vai || 37-36 ||

teṣāṃ tu tapasāṃ nyāsamatiriktaṃ tapaḥ śrutam |

namaḥśabdena kṛtātmanyāsasya svadhvaratvopapādanam

samitsādhanakādīnāṃ yajñānāṃ nyāsamātmanaḥ || 37-37 ||

namasā yo'karod deve sa svadhvara udīritaḥ |

nyāsasya paramadharmatve pramāṇapradarśanam

yāgasādhanabhūtena svātmanā vedyamīśvaram || 37-38 ||

ayajat tāni dharmāṇi prathamānīti naḥ śrutam |

nyāsasya sarvayajñasamatvam

yajñarūpadharaṃ devaṃ yajate svātmanaiva yaḥ || 37-39 ||

tena sarve kṛtā yajñā bhavantīha mahātmanā |

nyāsasya yajñatvanirvahaṇāya yajñāṅgapauṣkalyarūpaṇam

yajñarūpadharasyāsya śarīraṃ vediriṣyate || 37-40 ||

p. 359) āsyamāhavanīyāgnirhṛdayaṃ dakṣiṇānalaḥ |
athāsya gārhapatyāgnirudaraṃ śruticoditam || 37-41 ||

yajamāno manastattvaṃ buddhiḥ patnī prakīrtitā |
svāśritapratyanīkā ye paśavaste prakīrtitāḥ || 37-42 ||

lomāni barhiṣastvasya jīvaṃ havyaṃ pracakṣate |
savanāni śiromadhyagātrapādāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 37-43 ||

daśa yajñāyudhānyasya jñānakarmendriyāṇyapi |
ṛtvijaḥ ṣoḍaśa bhujā devasyāsya mahāmune || 37-44 ||

bhaktarakṣaṇasaṃkalpo dīkṣā devasya nārada |
ṛgyajuḥsāmaghoṣo'sya bhūṣaṇārāva iṣyate || 37-45 ||

sadasyā bhūṣaṇānyasya dayā devasya dakṣiṇā |
juhūrdhruvā sruvaścaiva prāśitraharaṇaṃ tathā || 37-46 ||

mekṣaṇopabhṛtau veda iḍāpātrī tathaiva ca |
dārupātraṃ ca yoktraṃ ca camasaḥ sṛṣṭireva ca || 37-47 ||

piṣṭodvapanyājyasthālyāvidhmapravraścanaṃ tathā |
madantītyasya śastreṣu cakrādyeṣu samāśritāḥ || 37-48 ||

prapannasya kṛtakṛtyatvanigamanam

evaṃrūpaṃ tamabhyarcya prakṣīṇāśeṣapātakaḥ |
anuṣṭhitakratuśato bhavedeṣa na saṃśayaḥ || 37-49 ||

p. 360) yathoktakrameṇa sudarśanamanārādhayatāṃ 
kleśaprāptinigamanam

evaṃ bahuvidhai rūpairupetaṃ taṃ sudarśanam |
kṛtvā tamapratiṣṭhāpya rājāno mantriṇo'pi vā || 37-50 ||

vinaṣṭasaṃpadaḥ sadyaḥ paribhūtāśca śatrubhiḥ | 
arcanābhāvato rājyād bhraṣṭāściramupadrutāḥ || 37-51 ||

sudarśanamanārādhayatāṃ rājye brahmahatyādidoṣotpattiḥ

svarājyaṃ brahmahatyādidoṣairduṣyenmahāmune |
samidityasya ṛgvedaśrutirāha (1) dayāvatī || 37-52 ||

sudarśanamanārādhayatāṃ rājñāṃ prajānurāgābhāvaḥ

anabhidhyānato hyasya viśve viṣayavāsinaḥ | 
aparaktāśca jāyante mahārāje kṛtāgasi || 37-53 ||

rājye sudarśanānārādhanāt prajānāmapi bahuduḥkhabhāgitā

aśraddadhānāścaitasminniha janmani duḥkhinaḥ |

_______________
 
1  atredaṃ ṛgvedaśrutidvayaṃ bodhyam- yaḥ samidhā ya āhutī yo vedena 
dadāśa marto agnaye | yo namasā svaghvaraḥ || tasyedarvanto raṃhayanta 
āśavastasya dyumnitamaṃ yaśaḥ | na tamaṃho devakṛtaṃ kutaścana na 
martyakṛtaṃ naśat || (ṛgvede 8-19, 6.)

_______________

p. 361) pretyādho'dhaḥ patantyeva nāpnuvanti sukhaṃ kvacit || 37-54 ||

sudarśanasamārādhanasya sarvaphalāvāptisādhanatānigamanam

tadyajñarūpaṃ susamāśritānāṃ
rakṣāvidhāne kṛtadīkṣamīśam |
subhīṣaṇaṃ ṣoḍaśabāhuyuktaṃ
svaśatrupakṣakṣapaṇapravṛttam || 37-55 ||

yathoktarūpaṃ dhṛtasarvaśastra-
mupetamastrairapi mūrtimadbhiḥ |
paṭe likhitvā śilayāthavārcāṃ
vidhāya lohairuta bimbamasya || 37-56 ||

samarcayedyo nṛpatiḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ
vidhāya bhaktyātha sudarśaneśam |
kṣaṇena sidhyanti manīṣitāni
vipakṣanāśaśca bhavedamuṣya || 37-57 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
nyāsāparaparyāyaprapattinirūpaṇaṃ nāma saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 37 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 2376



jvarādiroganivṛttyupāyavidhānaṃ nāma aṣṭātriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya taddivyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

uktānuvādapūrvakaṃ rogamocanopāyapraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

ākhyātaṃ bhavatā rājñāṃ parebhyo rakṣaṇaṃ param |
tathaiva sarve sarvajña puruṣārthāḥ samīritāḥ || 38-1 ||

tatsādhanāni cānyacca prasaṅgāt samudīritam |
idaṃ tvidānīṃ bhagavan vyākhyāhi mama pṛcchataḥ || 38-2 ||

rājñāṃ tatsacivānāṃ ca tathānyeṣāṃ nṛṇāmapi |
rogāḥ krūratarāsteṣāṃ puruṣārthavirodhinaḥ || 38-3 || 

saṃbhavanti hi teṣāṃ tu mocanaṃ kena sidhyati |

rogotpattitadvināśakāraṇakathanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mune rogā jāyante yena duḥsahāḥ || 38-4 ||

śṛṇu yenaiva naśyanti tad vakṣyāmi tavādhunā |

p. 363) prākṛtapralaye ekākinaḥ paramapuruṣasya ramaṇābhāvaḥ

prākṛte sarvalokasya saṃprāpte pratisaṃcare || 38-5 ||

brahmāyuṣi parikṣīṇe naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame |
sarvabhūteṣu bhūtādau pralīneṣu mahāmune || 38-6 ||

bhūtādāvindriyādau ca taijase mahati sthite |
tato mahati yāte tu prakṛtiṃ buddhilakṣaṇe || 38-7 ||

tathā ca puruṣe tasyāṃ pralīne pañcaviṃśake |
tasyāmapi pralīnāyāṃ puruṣe puṣkarekṣaṇe || 38-8 ||

sa eva sarvaṃ saṃhṛtya svasmin paramapūruṣaḥ |
bhagavān puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ paramātmā jaganmayaḥ || 38-9 ||

ekākī sa tadā naiva ramate sma sanātanaḥ |

līlārthaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ

sa līlārthaṃ punaścedamasṛjat puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ || 38-10 ||

nāmarūpavyākaraṇam

sa pūrvaṃ nāmarūpāṇi cakre sarvasya sarvagaḥ |

līlopakaraṇabhūtaprakṛtyā paramātmano ramaṇam

līlopakaraṇāṃ devaḥ prakṛtiṃ triguṇātmikām || 38-11 ||

māyāsaṃjñāṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭvā tayā reme janārdanaḥ |

prakṛtyā jīvaparayoḥ svarūpapracchādanam

sā tu sarvāṇi bhūtāni tattacchaktisamanvitā || 38-12 ||

p. 364) modayantī ca tanvānā svasyāṃ bhogyadhiyaṃ tathā | 
vartate jīvaparayoḥ svarūpācchādanāya sā || 38-13 ||

prakṛtipāravaśyājjīvasya saṃsaraṇam

tayā vivaśametattu saṃsaratyakhilaṃ jagat |

prakṛterdehātmabhramahetutvam

tayaiva prathamaṃ dehe karotyātmadhiyaṃ naraḥ || 38-14 ||

saivāsve svatvadhīhetuḥ

tataḥ śarīrasaṃbandhiputrādau mamatāṃ gataḥ |

ahaṃkāramamakārotthavāsanādārdhyam

tato'haṃkārajāstasya mamatotthāśca vāsanāḥ || 38-15 ||

bhavanti dehe putrādau dṛḍhabaddhāściraṃ mune |

tadutthavāsanānāṃ rāgadveṣadvārā puṇyapāpahetutvam

tataḥ svadehe putrādāvupakāryapakāriṣu || 38-16 ||

rāgadveṣau ca bhavatastābhyāṃ sarvaṃ viceṣṭate |
kapūyaramaṇīye ca tadutthe saṃcinotyayam || 38-17 ||

tato jātyāyurbhogarūpakarmavipākaprāptiḥ

tadvipākānathāpnoti jātyāyurbhogalakṣaṇān |

prārabdhakarmaphalopabhogāyaiva śarīrasṛṣṭiḥ

ataḥ phalopabhogārthamubhayeṣāṃ ca karmaṇām || 38-18 ||

p. 365) prārabdhānāmidaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ śarīraṃ ca śarīriṇaḥ |

rogāṇāṃ pāpajanyatvam

pāpmabhya eva jāyante prārabdheṣvāmayāstanau || 38-19 ||

nānāvidhā duḥkhavarā bhujyante dehināpare |

tatra prathamaṃ jvaraśāntyupāyaḥ

jvarābhibhūte nṛpatau sacive'nyajane'thavā || 38-20 ||

mahāsudarśanaṃ yantraṃ kathitaṃ mantrakovidaiḥ |
guḍūcīrājyasaṃsiktāścaturaṅgulasaṃmitāḥ || 38-21 ||

mantreṇānena juhuyāt sahasraṃ jvalite'nale |
cakrāmbujaṃ ca vinyasya sthaṇḍile śālitaṇḍulaiḥ || 38-22 ||

tatra pātraṃ nidhāyājyaṃ prasthapūrṇaṃ nidhāya ca |
tasyāṃ palapramāṇeṣu tantuṣvāropya dīpikām || 38-23 ||

tatrāpyenaṃ samāvāhya mantreṇānena mantravit |
tataḥ paścimabhāge tu sthaṇḍile maṇḍite śucau || 38-24 ||

āstīrya kalamāṃstatra nyasenmāhendramaṇḍalam |
tanmadhye ṣaḍaraṃ cakraṃ madhye tāraṃ ca vinyaset || 38-25 ||

tantujālena saṃveṣṭya navakumbhaṃ suśobhanam |
candracandanakāśmīramiśrairāpūrya vāribhiḥ || 38-26 ||

tadbahiścandanādyaistamarcayedakṣatairapi |
p. 366) ratnāni pañca padmaṃ ca dūrvāṃ hāṭakameva ca || 38-27 ||

śyāmākaṃ viṣṇuparṇīṃ ca devīṃ cāntarvinikṣipet |
madhyekumbhaṃ samāveṣṭya kṣaumeṇa navamālayā || 38-28 ||

taṃ tāramadhye saṃsthāpya sādhyanāmayute śubhe |
pūrṇacandramabhidhyāyaṃstatra cāvāhayet prabhum || 38-29 ||

taṃ dhūpadīpamālyādyaiḥ samabhyarcya yathāvidhi |
śālyannaṃ dadhimadhvājyasarvavyañjanasaṃyutam || 38-30 ||

hṛdyaṃ sājyaṃ guḍaṃ dadyād devāya haviruttamam |
baliṃ tato hared dikṣu vidikṣu ca samāhitaḥ || 38-31 ||

śālyannaiḥ śvetapuṣpaiśca dhūpadīpādibhistathā |
dikpālānāṃ tato dadyāt kṣetreśāyāpi nārada || 38-32 ||

āsane prāṅmukhaṃ sādhyamupaveśyātha sajvaram |
darpaṇaṃ tasya saṃsthāpya purastāt pratibimbitam || 38-33 ||

kumbhamādāya mantreṇa darpaṇaṃ so'bhiṣecayet |
sadya eva jvarānmukto bhavenmeghādivoḍurāṭ || 38-34 ||

atha dvijebhyo vidvadbhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo viśeṣataḥ |
niṣkatrayaṃ hiraṇyasya droṇaṃ cājyasya gāṃ tathā || 38-35 ||

karpūraṃ candanaṃ cāpi vāsāṃsi vimalānyapi |
etāni dadyādekasmai mune brahmavide'thavā || 38-36 ||

p. 367) tataḥ svastho bhavet sadyo yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
eṣā cikitsā prakṛtirvyādhīnāṃ mātrayā bhidā || 38-37 ||

rājayakṣmaśāntiḥ

rājayakṣmābhibhūte tu nṛpādau munisattama |
madhvaktābhirapāmārgasamidbhiḥ khādire'nale || 38-38 ||

mantreṇānena juhuyāt sahasraṃ mantravittamaḥ |
tataḥ pratīcye digbhāge pūrvavat kalpite'nale || 38-39 ||

āḍhakājyābhipūrṇāyāṃ pātryāṃ dvipalatantuṣu |
āropya dīpikāṃ paścād devamāvāhya mantrataḥ || 38-40 ||

tataḥ pāścāttyabhāge tu bhāraśālipariṣkṛte |

pūrvavat ṣaḍaraṃ cakraṃ vinyasyāreṣvamuṣya ca |
mantravarṇāni vinyasya sādhyanāma ca tārake || 38-42 ||

pūrvavanmantritaṃ kumbhaṃ tatra saṃsthāpya maṇḍale |
āvāhya devamabhyarcya pūrvavaccakrarūpiṇam || 38-43 ||

gurukṣetreśavighneśadurgādikpālakān prati |
prāvṛtya kambalenainaṃ sādhyaṃ prāṅmukhamāsane || 38-45 ||

p. 368) āsīnaṃ mantrataḥ kumbhavāribhiścābhiṣecayet |
anvahaṃ darpaṇāviṣṭaṃ snāpayecchāyayā punaḥ || 38-46 ||

niṣkadvayaṃ hiraṇyasya dviniṣkaṃ rajataṃ tathā |

kamaṇḍaluṃ kāṃsyapātraṃ chatraṃ copānahau tathā |
ekasmai brāhmaṇāyaiva viduṣe cāstikāya vai || 38-48 ||

dadyādetāni sarvāṇi sarvasatkārapūrvakam |
prayoktāraṃ dhanairevamabhyarcya prayato bhavet || 38-49 ||

sadyastasmānmahārogād vimukto nātra saṃśayaḥ |

prameharogaśāntiḥ

evaṃ prameharogeṇa gṛhīte ca mahāmune || 38-50 ||

abhiṣekaṃ svayaṃ kuryādanyat pūrvavadācaret |

ekaṃ cānaḍuhaṃ sadyo nīrogaḥ syānmahāmune |

p. 369) tatpūrvabhāge prajvālya jvalanaṃ kuṇḍamadhyame |

śuklaudanaiḥ śuklapuṣpaiḥ

kumbhādhivāsitaṃ devaṃ
pūjayed vidhivat tataḥ || 38-58 ||

havirnivedayecchuddhairodanaiḥ kalpitaṃ bahu |

tataḥ svastho bhavenmartyo devavanmodate'niśam |

tīvrāmayagṛhītāśca mahiṣyastasya bhūpateḥ || 42-18 ||

prabhavantyahivalmīkāḥ prāsāde dvāri maṇḍape |
nipatanti maholkāśca bhṛśaṃ bhīmasvanānvitāḥ || 42-19 ||

mantriṇaśca virudhyante matsareṇa parasparam |
rajanyāṃ rājate bhīmamaindraṃ dhanuranabhrajam || 42-20 ||

itastato vahnibhayaṃ nagare jāyate bhṛśam |
praviśya garbhabhavanaṃ kroṣṭāraścānivāritāḥ || 42-21 ||

krośanti saṃdhyayorbhīmā dīptāyāṃ diśi visvaram |
rundhanti nagaraṃ rājñaḥ śatravo baladarpitāḥ || 42-22 ||

kṛtyākṛtyaṃ na jānāti svayaṃ staimityamāsthitaḥ |
svapne'pi paśyatyātmānaṃ muṇḍitaṃ nīlavāsasam || 42-23 ||

tathena gardabhayujā vrajantaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ diśam |
ityādiliṅgairjānīyādabhicāraṃ sapatnajam || 42-24 ||

parābhicārajā kṛtyā rājānaṃ praviśedyadi |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā kṣipramevāsau vinaśyati na saṃśayaḥ || 42-25 ||

putrāṃśca mantriṇaścāpi mahiṣīṃ nagaraṃ tathā |
jvālāmālāvilā kṛtyā sarvaṃ nāśayati kṣaṇāt || 42-26 ||

parābhicāranivartanāya nivartakāstradhārisudarśanaprīṇanam

evaṃ parābhicāreṇa pīḍite nṛpatau svayam |

p. 404) nivartakāstrasahitaṃ bhagavantaṃ mahādyutim || 42-27 ||

cakrasvarūpiṇaṃ devaṃ bhaktyā saṃtoṣayet tataḥ |
rajatena suvarṇena śilayā tāmrakeṇa vā || 42-28 ||

sudarśanārcāṃ nirmāya pūjayet sanivartakam |
samālikhya paṭe vāpi dhyāyet trailokyapūjitam || 42-29 ||

evaṃ nirmāṇato dhyānāt pūjayā vandanena vā |
pratiṣṭhayā vā stotreṇa devaṃ saṃprīṇayennṛpaḥ || 42-30 ||

evamanye tathā kuryuramātyā vā narā mune |

prītena sudarśanena parābhicāranirdahanam

tataḥ prītaḥ sa bhagavānanādinidhano vibhuḥ || 42-31 ||

abhicārān parakṛtānāśritānugraheṇa tu |
bhasmīkaroti smṛtyaiva tatprayuktānyapi kṣaṇāt || 42-32 ||

divyāstrāṇyupasaṃhārairastrairvārayatīśvaraḥ |

paraprayuktakṛtyāyāstatprayoktuśca vināśanam

paraprayuktāṃ kṛtyāṃ tu saṃprāptāmatibhīṣaṇām || 42-33 ||

svayaṃ nirgatya devo'sau cakrarūpī jagatpatiḥ |
bhasmasāt kurute taṃ ca prayoktāraṃ tataḥ param || 42-34 ||

kāśīrājetihāsodāharaṇam

tathaiva kāśīrājākhyo vārāṇasyāṃ vasannṛpaḥ |
viśveśvaraṃ mahādevamarcayitvā samāhitaḥ || 42-35 ||

p. 405) kṛṣṇoddeśena kṛtyāṃ vai janayāmāsa mohitaḥ |
sā samutpāditā kṛtyā vikaṭā bhīmadarśanā || 42-36 ||

dvārakāṃ samanuprāpya kṛṣṇābhyāsaṃ yayau tadā |
tāmāpatantīṃ saṃprekṣya jvālāmālāvilānanām || 42-37 ||

preṣayāmāsa bhagavān sudarśanamanuttamam |
sā tamāpatitaṃ dṛṣṭvā sahasrādityasaṃnibham || 42-38 ||

yathāgataṃ pradudrāva prayoktāraṃ bhayāturā |
sa tāṃ nihatya taṃ cāpi kāśīrājaṃ purīṃ tathā || 42-39 ||

ājagāma punaścakraṃ viṣṇupārśvaṃ mahādyuti |

śrutakīrtyupākhyānodāharaṇam

evamanyat purāvṛttamitihāsaṃ śṛṇuṣva me || 42-40 ||

rājñā śrutakīrtinā dharmeṇa saptadvīpamahīparipālanam

saurāṣṭrādhipatī rājā śrutakīrtiriti śrutaḥ |
nivasan bhadraśālāyāṃ nagaryāmarimardanaḥ || 42-41 ||

gajāśvarathasaṃpanno balavān vāsavopamaḥ |
sudarśanamahāmantraniṣṭhaḥ paramadhārmikaḥ || 42-42 ||

sudarśane parāṃ bhaktimudvahannarcanāparaḥ |
bubhuje tatprasādena saptadvīpavatīṃ mahīm || 42-43 ||

p. 406) lokāntaraṃ jigīṣostasya sudarśanārcanapūrvakaṃ 
svastikākhyagandharvanagaragamanam

jetuṃ lokāntaraṃ cakre śrutakīrtirmanastadā |
caturaṅgabalairyuktaḥ samabhyarcya sudarśanam || 42-44 ||

kāmagaṃ syandanaṃ citramāruroha mahābalaḥ |
tataḥ pratasthe prathamamantarikṣaṃ balānvitaḥ || 42-45 ||

svastikanagaravarṇanam

tatra gandharvanagaraṃ viśālaṃ svastikāhvayam |
vīṇāvinodanenetthaṃ pālitaṃ paramadyuti || 42-46 ||

svayaṃpravṛttaiḥ śastraiśca vāryamāṇārisainikam |
bahirnirindhanenaiva vahninā pariveṣṭitam || 42-47 ||

suvarṇasālaṃ paramaṃ ratnagopuramaṇḍitam |
maṇiharmyaparikṣiptaṃ gānasvanasamākulam || 42-48 ||

vimānaistaijasairjuṣṭaṃ divyopavanaśobhitam |
suvarṇapadmasarasīsaridvāpīniṣevitam || 42-49 ||

sevitaṃ suranārībhirapsarobhiśca sevitam |
gandharvaiḥ kiṃnaraiḥ siddhaiścāraṇaiśca sadā yutam || 42-50 ||

yuyutsunā śrutakīrtinā tannagaroparodhaḥ

evaṃvidhaṃ puraṃ rājā rurodha baladarpitaḥ |
vīṇāvinodanaṃ yoddhuṃ mahatyā senayā tadā || 42-51 ||

p. 407) 		gandharvarājena vāhinīpreṣaṇam

śrutvā gandharvarājo'pi mānuṣairuparodhanam |
prahasya preṣayāmāsa siddhagandharvavāhinīm || 42-52 ||

tatra tumulasaṃgrāmaḥ

sā tena balinā rājñā yuyudhe niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ |
pravṛttaṃ tanmahadyuddhaṃ tumulaṃ romaharṣaṇam || 42-53 ||

śrutakīrtinā gandharvasainyavidrāvaṇam

tato vidrāvayāmāsa bhūpatirlīlayā balam |
gāndharvaṃ tat punaḥ prāpa svapurīṃ bhayavihvalam || 42-54 ||

kruddhasya gandharvarājasya svayaṃ yuddhāya nirgamanam

tacchrutvā paramakruddhaḥ svayaṃ vīṇāvinodanaḥ |
rathaṃ mahāntamāsthāya niryayau saha sainikaiḥ || 42-55 ||

gandharvarājaśrutakīrtyoraṣṭadinaparyantaṃ yuddhapravṛttiḥ

tatastayorabhūdyuddhaṃ vṛtravāsavayoriva |
parasparaṃ nijaghnuste sainikāḥ vividhaiḥ śaraiḥ || 42-56 ||

tatra saṃchannamākāśaṃ śaraughairubhayorapi |
jayaḥ parājayo vā syānna tatra samitau tayoḥ || 42-57 ||

evamaṣṭadinānyāsīdekarūpaṃ bhayaṃkaram |

p. 408) bhūpatinā gandharvarājasya dhanurādicchedo balavidrāvaṇaṃ ca

tato gandharvarājasya ghanuściccheda bhūpatiḥ || 42-58 ||

dhvajacchatrahayāṃścāpi sāyakairnataparvabhiḥ |
diśaḥ prasthāpayāmāsa tadīyaṃ sakalaṃ balam || 42-59 ||

gandharvarājasya viṣādaḥ

śastrairajayyamālocya nṛpaṃ gandharvarāḍapi |
parājitaṃ tu svabalaṃ manujairviṣasāda ha || 42-60 ||

gandharvarājena mānuṣabale gāndharvāstraprayogaḥ

divyāstraiḥ sūdayāmyenamiti matvā mahādyutiḥ |
gāndharvamastraṃ cikṣepa sa tasmin mānuṣe bale || 42-61 ||

tena mānuṣasainyavimohanam

tenaiva mohitā sarvā śrutakīrtivarūthinī |
gāndharvāstraprabhāveṇa tiṣṭhatyeva ca saṃyuge || 42-62 ||

sudarśanaprabhāveṇa divyāstraṃ nāpa taṃ nṛpam |

tatpratīkārāśaktasya śrutakīrteḥ purodhasi svavṛttanivedanam

śrutakīrtistadāstreṣu divyeṣvanabhiyogataḥ || 42-63 ||

na śaśāka pratīkartuṃ svapurodhasamabhyayāt |
vṛttaṃ vijñāpayāmāsa nṛpastasmai purodhase || 42-64 ||

purodhasā sudarśanaśaraṇavaraṇopadeśapūrvakaṃ 
nikhilāstramantropadeśanam

so'bhyabhāṣata dharmajñaḥ paramāstraviśāradaḥ |

p. 409) śrutakīrte'tra mā bhaiṣīstameva śaraṇaṃ vraja || 42-65 ||

praṇatārtiharaṃ devaṃ sudarśanavapurdharam |
divyāstrāṇāmaśeṣāṇāṃ prasūtiṃ parameśvaram || 42-66 ||

evaṃ bhūte mahācakre catuḥṣaṣṭyarasaṃyute |
nemidvayānvite devaṃ madhye dhyāyasva bhūpate || 42-67 ||

dviṣaṣṭibhujamatyugraṃ saṃhārāstradharaṃ param |
evaṃ dhyātvā ca japtvaitaṃ mantraṃ senāṃ vilokaya || 42-68 ||

ityastramantrānakhilāṃstasmā upadideśa saḥ |

tena jātabalasya śrutakīrteḥ punaryuddhāyāgamanam

pratigṛhyākhilān mantrān pravartakanivartakān || 42-69 ||

punaretya ca saṃgrāmaṃ sa rājā samitiṃjayaḥ |

tena svasainikamohavimocanam

dhyātvā devaṃ japan mantraṃ svasenāṃ samalokayat || 42-70 ||

tataḥ saṃjñāmavāpyāsau prāvartata mahāhave |

punaryuddhe śrutakīrtervijayo gandharvalokavaśīkaraṇaṃ ca

punaḥ pravavṛte yuddhaṃ senayorubhayorapi || 42-71 ||

divyāstraiḥ samare rājā jitvā vīṇāvinodanam |
tallokaṃ svavaśaṃ cakre cakravartī balānvitaḥ || 42-72 ||

p. 410) 		tataḥ svapurapratyāgamanam

satkṛtaḥ siddhagandharvaiḥ kiṃnaraiścāraṇairapi |
bhadraśālāṃ punaḥ prāpa svapurīṃ vijayaśriyā || 42-73 ||

sameyivāñśaśāsorvīṃ divaṃ surapatiryathā |

sudarśanaprabhāvādvismitasya punaḥ purodhasā saha mantraṇaprakāraḥ

sudarśanaprabhāvasya smṛtvā vismitamānasaḥ || 42-74 ||

purodhasamupāgamya jvalantamiva pāvakam |
mantrayāmāsa tenaivaṃ rājā rājīvalocanaḥ || 42-75 ||

svasaṃpado bhagavatprasādahetukatvakathanam

devaprasādājjagatī mameyaṃ
samudranemirvaśamāgatādya |
lokāntaraṃ cāpi tathaiva manye
mayi prasādonmukha eva devaḥ || 42-76 ||

bhagavatprasādāt svasya mokṣalābhe saṃśayābhāvakathanam

saṃsārabhītasya mamāpavargaṃ
pradāsyate cakravapurdharo'yam |
ityatra śaṅkā mama nāsti kāci-
dataḥ paraṃ devamupāsmahe tu || 42-77 ||

p. 411) rājānaṃ prati purodhasā sudarśanapratiṣṭhāpanādyupadeśaḥ

ityuktavantaṃ nṛpatiṃ purodhāḥ
kṛtāñjaliṃ prāha mahānubhāvaḥ |
vimānamagryaṃ bahuvarṇakūṭaṃ
vidhāya cakropapadaṃ sasādhyam || 42-78 ||

iha pratiṣṭhāpya sucakramadhye
dviṣaṣṭibāhuṃ sanivartakāstram |
yathoktarūpaṃ bahubhūṣaṇāḍhyaṃ
sudarśanaṃ pūjaya sarvasiddhyai || 42-79 ||

rājñā tathaivānuṣṭhānam

ityevamuktaḥ pratigṛhya mūrdhnā
sa tasya vākyaṃ paramārthayuktam |
akārayat kārayitavyadakṣa-
stathaiva tasminnagare sa rājā || 42-80 ||

tadarthaṃ rājñā dvijānāṃ vṛttiprakalpanam

śataṃ sa viṃśatyadhikaṃ dvijāṃstu
samantato devaniketanāt saḥ |
tataḥ pratiṣṭhāpya dhanāni dattvā
bahūni bahvīśca bhuvaḥ sa tebhyaḥ || 42-81 ||

p. 412) vibhūtimantaṃ ca vidhāya devaṃ
mumoda rājā samavāptakāmaḥ |

tato bhagavatprasādādrājñaḥ paramapuruṣārthalābhaḥ

mahālaye tatra samarcya devaṃ
dine dine prāptasamastakāmaḥ |
tato'cireṇaiva vimuktasaṅgaḥ
paraṃ padaṃ prāpa ca tatprasādāt || 42-82 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
kāśīrājaśrutakīrtyupākhyānavarṇanaṃ nāma  
dvicatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 42 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 2673



jalaṃdharavadhopāyacintanaṃ nāma tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

kṛtsnasya jagaccakramayatvakathaṃtāpraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

pṛcchāmi tvāṃ punaridaṃ śaṃsa me sakaleśvara |
proktametat purā sarvaṃ jagaccakramayaṃ tvayā || 43-1 ||

tat kathaṃ spaṣṭamācakṣva kadā devena darśitam |
vettā tvameva nānyo'sti tvadṛte bhuvanatraye || 43-2 ||

adhyāyadvayena taduttarakathanāyākhyāyikākathane prathamaṃ 
devādīnāṃ dharmamārgapravartakatvanirūpaṇam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

surāśca munayaḥ sarve manustatsūnavastathā |
rājānaḥ sarvavarṇāśca dharmamārgapravartakāḥ || 43-3 ||

dānavādīnāṃ dharmavighātakatvam

p. 414) tathaiva dānavāḥ sarve bhūtavetālarākṣasāḥ |
krūrā narāśca paśavo bhīmarūpāśca dasyavaḥ || 43-4 ||

tathaiva cāsurīṃ prāptā yonimanye janā mune |
ete sarve sadā dharmavidhvaṃsaṃ kurvate bhṛśam || 43-5 ||

pāpināṃ vināśopāyacintayāhirbudhnyasya meruśikharagamanam

ataste pāpakarmāṇaḥ kena naśyeyurityaham |
parāṃ cintāmupārūḍho merukūṭamupāgataḥ || 43-6 ||

tatra samādhimāsthitasya tasya tejoviśeṣadarśanam

tasmin samādhimāsthāya samāsīnasya me kṣaṇāt |
āvirbabhūva durdarśaṃ mahaḥ paramabhāsvaram || 43-7 ||

bhītivaśāt ātharvaṇamantroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ punastaddarśanam

tatastaddarśanānmantramātharvaṇamudairayam |
tamuccaran mahāmantramadrākṣaṃ tanmahaḥ punaḥ || 43-8 ||

tejomadhye sudarśanapuruṣasākṣātkārapūrvakaṃ praśnaḥ

tasmin vidīpte mahasi puruṣaṃ kṛṣṇapiṅgalam |
ūrdhvaretaṃ virūpākṣaṃ sahasrādityasaṃnibham || 43-9 ||

sahasrārasamāyuktacakramadhyasthitaṃ param |
jvālāmālāsudurdarśaṃ sudarśanamanākulam || 43-10 ||

p. 415) dṛṣṭvā kastvaṃ kutaḥ kasyetyapṛcchamakutobhayam |

prītena bhagavatā svasvarūpakhyāpanam

tataḥ prītaḥ sa bhagavānavadat tejasāṃ nidhiḥ || 43-11 ||

ahaṃ sudarśano nāmnā khyāto'dya prabhṛtīśvara |
durāloko mahāloko yat tavāhaṃ sudarśanaḥ || 43-12 ||

vaikuṇṭhāt svāgamanasyāhirbudhnyānujighṛkṣāhetukatvādikathanam

vaikuṇṭhadhāmanilayastvāṃ draṣṭuṃ samupāgataḥ |
tribhāvabhāvanāyuktairadṛṣṭacaramadbhutam || 43-13 ||

nityamantarhitaṃ guhyaṃ sarvalokanamaskṛtam |
pramathya matimanthānāduddhṛtaṃ vedasāgarāt || 43-14 ||

pīyūṣamiva dugdhābdheryat tvayā parameśvara |
ato mantratanuḥ prāpto bhavantaṃ kiṃ karomi te || 43-15 ||

antaraṃ naiva paśyāmi tava dāmodarasya ca |
ato yathātra varteya tathaiva tvayi śaṃkara || 43-16 ||

tato'hirbudhnyena bhagavati svābhipretanivedanam

ityuktvā virate tasmin sahasrādityasaṃnibhe |
tato'haṃ vismito'vocaṃ tamapratimatejasam || 43-17 ||

bhūyāṃso bhuvane pāpā dharmavyāsedhakāriṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ nigrahamākāṅkṣannupāyairnāśayāmyaham || 43-18 ||

p. 416) iti cintāpare yogamāsthite mayi mantrarāṭ |
tvaṃ cāvirāsīstatkāryaṃ nirvṛttamiti me matiḥ || 43-19 ||

daityādivināśanaprārthanā

atastaiḥ pīḍyamāneṣu sādhuṣu smṛtimātrataḥ |
āgantavyaṃ prayatnena sādhyaṃ sādhaya ca prabho || 43-20 ||

bhagavatā tathetyukte svasya kailāsagamanam

tathetyuktvā gataḥ so'hamapi kailāsamanvagām |

atrāntare jalaṃdharavadhārthamindrasya mantrālocanā

tato jalaṃdharākhyena dānavena nipīḍitaḥ || 43-21 ||

vāsavo mantrayāṃcakre saha sarvairmarudgaṇaiḥ |
śastrairastrairajayyatvaṃ dānavasya durātmanaḥ || 43-22 ||

nirīkṣya kena taṃ pāpaṃ nāśayāmītyacodayat |

tatra vāyunā svābhiprāyavinivedanam

tato vāyuḥ samutthāya puraṃdaramathābravīt || 43-23 ||

satyamastraistathā śastrairajayyo'yaṃ mahāsuraḥ |
dhātrā dattavaro dṛpto balavīryasamanvitaḥ || 43-24 ||

didhakṣati jagat sarvaṃ dhūmaketurivotthitaḥ |
sarvalokeśvare śrīmatyaprameye mahādyutau || 43-25 ||

devadeve mahādeve śaṃkare bhūtabhāvane |
tasminnatyāhitaṃ tena nṛśaṃsena surāriṇā || 43-26 ||

p. 417) tasmin paramasaṃkruddho vartate tena śaṃkaraḥ |
atastamabhigacchāmo vadhārthaṃ duṣṭacāriṇaḥ || 43-27 ||

bṛhaspatimukhenaiva taṃ devaṃ prārthayāmahe |
sa eva tatra vaktavyaṃ jānāti bhagavāniti || 43-28 ||

vāyuvacanamaṅgīkṛtya śaṃkaraṃ gantuṃ śakreṇa bṛhaspatipracodanam

virate pavane tasmin bāḍhamityāha vāsavaḥ |
bṛhaspatimathāsīnaṃ jvalitānalasaṃnibham || 43-29 ||

abhyagacchanmahātejā bhagavān pākaśāsanaḥ |
jalaṃdharavadhārthaṃ tvamabhigaccha pinākinam || 43-30 ||

sa tasmin paramakruddho nihaniṣyatyasaṃśayam |
surakāryeṣu sarveṣu bhavāneva parāyaṇam || 43-31 ||

ato'bhigamyatāṃ devaḥ kailāse candraśekharaḥ |

indravacanāt bṛhaspateḥ kailāsagamanam

ityuktavacane tasmin puruhūte sureśvare || 43-32 ||

tatheti tat pratiśrutya vākpatiḥ śakrapūjitaḥ |
kailāsaśikharaṃ prāyāt prahṛṣṭenāntarātmanā || 43-33 ||

tatra śaṃkaradarśanam

tatrāsīnaṃ suraiḥ sārdhamāsthāne sumanohare |

p. 418) māmapsaraḥprayukte tu nṛtye vismitamānasam || 43-34 ||

so'paśyadārādbhagavānudyadbhāskarasaṃnibhaḥ |
praṇipatya samutthāya māmastauṣīt sa vākpatiḥ || 43-35 ||

jalaṃdharavadhārthaṃ bṛhaspatinā śaṃkarastutiḥ

prasīda devadeveśa prasīda jagataḥ pate |
prasīda sarvagīrvāṇapālanāntakara prabho || 43-36 ||

prasīda bhagavan deva prasīda suravandita |
prasīda viśvavijñānavāridhe parameśvara || 43-37 ||

namaste vyomanilaya namaste nāgabhūṣaṇa |
namaḥ paramakalyāṇa namastripuradāraṇa || 43-38 ||

tvaṃ dhātā tvaṃ vaṣaṭkārastvaṃ havistvaṃ hutāśanaḥ |
sadasattvaṃ tvamoṃkārastvamindrastvaṃ yamastathā || 43-39 ||

tvaṃ pāśapāṇistvaṃ somo nir-ṛtistvaṃ marut tathā |
tvamādityo vasuśca tvaṃ tvaṃ viśvaṃ viśvatomukha || 43-40 ||

tvamavyaktaṃ mahāṃśca tvaṃ tvamahaṃkāra eva ca |
bhūtāni tvaṃ susūkṣmāṇi vidyāvidye tathā bhavān || 43-41 ||

kimatra bahunoktena carācaramidaṃ jagat |
tvameva bhūtanātheśa sarvakāraṇakāraṇa || 43-42 ||

p. 419) kṣitisalilasamīravyomatejaḥsahasra-

paramamunimanobhirmṛgyamāṇāya nityaṃ

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ

bṛhaspatinā śaṃkarāya surakāryanivedanam

atastridaśakāryārthaṃ tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ gataḥ || 44-3 ||

p. 421) mahāsuro mahādevādṛte nāsau vinaśyati || 44-6 ||

sarvārthasādhako nityaḥ sarvaduṣṭabhayaṃkaraḥ |

svavidyārakṣaṇarataḥ paravidyānivāraṇaḥ |
p. 422) ātharvaṇo'pi ṣaṭkārṇaḥ ṣaṭkarṇaśrutyagocaraḥ || 44-14 ||

tenaiva taṃ durātmānamapaneṣyāmi mā ciram |

p. 423) cakravartī sa bhagavān saumyarūpaḥ sudarśanaḥ |

p. 432) 	kuśadhvajasya mahāmohena bhṛśaṃ pīḍā

svaprabhāveṇa taddoṣaṃ śaktaḥ śamayituṃ muniḥ |
tathāpi rājadharmo'yamiti buddhyā vicintya saḥ || 45-40 ||

jagāma maṇḍapaṃ tatra śuklapakṣe śubhe dine |

maṇḍapasya paścimabhāge rājāsanakalpanam

śilpinaḥ sa samāhūya rājāsanamakalpayat || 45-41 ||

asya paścimadigbhāge tṛtīye samalaṃkṛte |

prāgbhāgadvayamadhye homakuṇḍakalpanam

prāgbhāgadvayamadhye'sya homakuṇḍānyakārayat || 45-42 ||

vimānalakṣaṇopetavedikāsthalamūrdhasu |
cakrākṛtīni kuṇḍāni kalpayāmāsa sādaram || 45-43 ||

madhye aṣṭadikṣu ca kuṇḍakalpanā

tādṛśaṃ kuṇḍayugmaṃ tu madhye kṛtvā tato bahiḥ |
aṣṭāsu dikṣu kuṇḍāni tathaiva samakārayat || 45-44 ||

pratikuṇḍamekaikasya, madhye dvayośca kumbhayoḥ sthāpanam

pratikuṇḍaṃ paristīrya kalamān bhārasaṃmitān |
sthāpayāmāsa kuṇḍānāṃ pūrvapārśveṣu sarvataḥ || 45-45 ||

p. 437) kumbhāni rājatānyaṣṭau sauvarṇe madhyame ubhe |
gandhatoyena pūrṇāni navaratnayutāni ca || 45-46 ||

yājñavalkyena svayaṃ kumbhārcanam

tataḥ svayaṃ muniḥ sārdhaṃ śatānandena dhīmatā |
kṣaumaistantubhirāveṣṭya gandhapuṣpaistathākṣataiḥ || 45-47 ||

alaṃkṛtya ca kumbheṣu devamāvāhya sādaram |
arcayāmāsa mantreṇa dhūpadīpaprasūnakaiḥ || 45-48 ||

yantrapratiṣṭhāvilekhanādi

mahāyantraṃ pratiṣṭhāpya madhyekumbhadvayaṃ guruḥ |
devaṃ vinā mahāyantraṃ sarvamantrasamanvitam || 45-49 ||

paṭe vilikhya pūrvoktaṃ sarvadivyāstrasaṃyutam |
rājāsane vikīryaitat tatastat sottaracchadam || 45-50 ||

āropayāmāsa tadā rājānamamitaujasam |
sa yantrapaṭasaṃyuktaṃ vitānaṃ tasya cākarot || 45-51 ||

nirmāya ca suvarṇena maṇijālapariṣkṛtam |
yathoktalakṣaṇaṃ yantraṃ darpaṇe ca nyaveśayat || 45-52 ||

tathaiva gorocanayā bhūrjapatre susaṃskṛte |
suvarṇasūcyā saṃlikhya yantraṃ divyāstravarjitam || 45-53 ||

p. 438) śirasyadhārayadrājño yantrametat sumantritam | 
saudarśanena tilakaṃ bhasmanā cāpyakārayat || 45-54 ||

tataścāṣṭau samāhūya ṛtvijo mantraniṣṭhitān |
saṃyatān vaiṣṇavān viprān diśākuṇḍeṣvayojayat || 45-55 ||

tato yavanikāṃ kṛtvā maṇḍapasya samantataḥ |
alaṃkṛteṣu kuṇḍeṣu sauvarṇaiśca paricchadaiḥ || 45-56 ||

vidhāya vihitaṃ pūrvamṛtvigbhiḥ sahito guruḥ |
śatānandena sahito juhāva jvaliteṣu saḥ || 45-57 ||

nṛttavāditrasaṃyuktaṃ brahmaghoṣasamāyutam |
puṇyāhaṃ pūrvamakaroddhomakarma samāhitaḥ || 45-58 ||

pradhānakarmaṇo yuktau śatānando gurustathā |
diśāhomeṣu saṃyuktā ṛtvijaḥ sarva eva te || 45-59 ||

ājyairviśuddhairniyutaṃ homān saṃkalpya sādaram |
akarodayutaṃ nityamevaṃ daśadinaṃ kṛtam || 45-60 ||

guruṇānudinaṃ snāto mantritaiḥ kumbhavāribhiḥ |
muktagraho viśuddhātmā vijvaraḥ prītamānasaḥ || 45-61 ||

pūrṇe daśadine rājā ratnairbahusuvarṇakaiḥ |
ārādhayāmāsa guruṃ maṇḍapaṃ saparicchadam || 45-62 ||

nivedya gurave sarvānṛtvijaḥ samatarpayat |
dhanaiśca bahubhirdhānyaiḥ pratiṣṭhābhiḥ samāhitaḥ || 45-63 ||

p. 439) praśaśāsa mahīmenāṃ divi devendravat sudhīḥ |
anyeṣāmapi bhūtānāmayameva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || 45-64 ||

pañcāśanniyutairhomairdvāpare sa vidhīyate |
kalau ca koṭihomena vidhireṣa mahīkṣitām |
prārabdhamevaṃ bhoktavyaṃ karma brahmavidāmapi || 45-65 ||

iti śrīpañcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
kuśadhvajopākhyānavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 45 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 2837



sudarśanahomāṅgadravyaparimāṇādinirūpaṇaṃ nāma 
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

rājño homamaṇḍapānāgamane kartavyakramaḥ

nāradaḥ �

daivādvā mānuṣādvāpi bhūtādibhirathāpi vā |
darpāt pratibalādvānyairantarāyairmahīpatiḥ || 46-1 ||

yadi na prāpnuyādeva homamaṇḍapamāditaḥ |
tataḥ kathaṃ mahībharturnivartyante mahāgrahāḥ || 46-2 ||

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

śṛṇu rājā na cet kuryāt purodhāḥ kurutāt kṛtī |
sa eva rājñaḥ sarvasvamadṛṣṭārthopapādane || 46-3 ||

purohitalakṣaṇam

dhārmikaḥ śrutisaṃpannaḥ suśīlaḥ satyavāk śuciḥ |
abhijāto'nahaṃkārastitikṣuḥ smṛtimān vaśī || 46-4 ||

deśakālavibhāgajñaḥ śāstradṛṣṭiratandritaḥ |

p. 441) apradhṛṣyo'pramādī ca vadānyo nayakovidaḥ || 46-5 ||

upāyopeyavinmantrī yāyajūko hyalolupaḥ |
daivavit priyavādī ca vaidikaḥ sattvavān prabhuḥ || 46-6 ||

viṣṇubhaktastapasvī ca kāryavit karmaṭho'naghaḥ |
hitāhitāptihānecchurnṛpāṇāṃ sarvasaṃmataḥ || 46-7 ||

purodhasa eva rājñaḥ sarvakāryakartṛtvam

īdṛśo durlabho rājñāṃ gurukalpaḥ purohitaḥ |
īdṛśo hi kṣamo rājñāmaghaughavinivāraṇe || 46-8 ||

ataḥ sa eva rājñāṃ hi rakṣāvidhimathārhati |
evaṃvidho gururyasya sa samrād nṛpatirbhavet || 46-9 ||

dīrghāyurniḥsapatnaḥ syādarogaḥ paravīrahā |
avagrahādyā jāyante pīḍāstadviṣaye na hi || 46-10 ||

tādṛśapurohitābhāve rājño'niṣṭakathanam

taṃ vinānyo bhavedrājño gururvātha purohitaḥ |
viparītaṃ bhavet tasya mahībharturna saṃśayaḥ || 46-11 ||

kumbhādīnāṃ parimāṇādipraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

parimāṇaṃ ca kumbhānāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca lakṣaṇam |
ṛtvijaḥ kīdṛśo hyasya prakāraścākhilaṃ vada || 46-12 ||

p. 442) 		uttamaparimāṇam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

śuddhairgandhodakairbhāraparimāṇaiḥ supūritān |
sauvarṇarājatān kumbhān kārayedidamuttamam || 46-13 ||

madhyamaparimāṇam

ardhabhārabhṛtān kumbhān madhyamo'yaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |

adhamaparimāṇam

tadardhenādhame pakṣe pūrayedeṣa nirṇayaḥ || 46-14 ||

yathāvidhi srugādinirmāṇam

yathāvidhi vidheyau tu sruksruvau munisattama |

ṛtviksaṃkhyā

ṛtvijaḥ sāttvikānaṣṭau vṛṇuyādvaiṣṇāvāṃstataḥ || 46-15 ||

aṅgamantrādividhiḥ

aṅgamantraiśca gāyatryā vahniprākārakeṇa ca |
pradhānakuṇḍayoścāpi gurukalpau samāhitau || 46-16 ||

vṛṇuyānmūlamantreṇa sarve rakṣāmanau ratāḥ |
śamīsamidbhirdūrvābhistilairāmradalaistathā || 46-17 ||

caruṇā pāyasāpūpaiḥ pālāśairjuhuyāt kramāt |

p. 443) śuddhena sarpiṣā madhyakuṇḍayorhāvayed guruḥ || 46-18 ||

athavā sarvakuṇḍeṣu śuddhenājyena hāvayet |
guruṇaivābhinirvartye pratīkāravidhāviha || 46-19 ||

śayanāsanayānādau rājñaḥ svairavidhāvapi |
pūrvoktena vidhānena vitānādikamācaret || 46-20 ||

homānte rājño'bhiṣekaḥ

homāvasānasamaye prayoge'pyatra tadguruḥ |
tatra rājānamāhūya pūrvavanmantritāsane || 46-21 ||

sthāpayitvābhiṣekādisaskāraṃ gururācaret |

gurvādiprīṇanam

tataḥ svasthamanāḥ sadyo vimuktagrahabandhanaḥ || 46-22 ||

yathārhaṃ gurave dadyāt svacihnāni praṇamya ca |
bhūṣaṇāni mahārhāṇi muktāmaṇimayāni ca || 46-23 ||

maṇipīṭhasuvarṇāni ratnāni vividhāni ca |
bhuvaśca bhūyasīrdatvā ṛtvijastarpayet tataḥ || 46-24 ||

ekaikamṛtvijaḥ sarve śatenārcyāḥ suvarṇakaiḥ |
pratyekaṃ daśabhirgobhirvāsobhirvividhairapi || 46-25 ||

pradhānahomakartre viśeṣato dakṣiṇādānam

pradhānahomakartre tu deśikāya viśeṣataḥ |

p. 444) dadyād bahu dhanaṃ pūrṇamekamaśvaṃ savāhanam || 46-26 ||

alaṃkāreṇa sahitaṃ maṇḍapaṃ saparicchadam |

tato brāhmaṇatarpaṇam

brāhmaṇānāṃ tatastṛptiṃ kuryāt prītamanā nṛpaḥ || 46-27 ||

rājñā svenābhinirvṛtte karmaṇyasmin yathodite |
prayoge deśikāyaiva dadyānmantravide vasu || 46-28 ||

purodhasā kṛtaṃ sarvameṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ |
rājodāharaṇe tāvat saviśeṣamudīritam || 46-29 ||

anyeṣāmapi sarveṣāmeṣa eva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kuryādvaiṣṇavyarcā samāhitaḥ |
rājño'bhyudayamākāṅkṣan deśiko mantravittamaḥ || 46-30 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
sudarśanahomāṅgadravyaparimāṇādinirūpaṇaṃ nāma 
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 46 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 2867



mahāśāntividhānaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

aihikāmuṣmikasarvaphalasādhakaikopāyapraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

bhagavan pañcamantrāṅga paśupāśahara prabho |
svataḥ sākṣātkṛtāśeṣaviśeṣa vyomamandira || 47-1 ||

aihikāmuṣmikaṃ śaśvanmantreṇānena labhyate |
ubhaye'pi tathā doṣā nāśyante nātra saṃśayaḥ || 47-2 ||

ityuktaṃ tena pṛcchāmi yenaikenaiva karmaṇā |
ubhayī siddhirubhayāniṣṭanāśo bhavet tathā || 47-3 ||

īdṛśaṃ karma cedasti tanme kathaya śaṃkara |
īdṛggrahasya tvatto'nyo na kaścid vaktumarhati || 47-4 ||

taduttarakathanārambhaḥ

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

tadetat kathayiṣyāmi sarvaśāntikaraṃ param |

vakṣyamāṇe karmaviśeṣe rājñāmadhikāraḥ

mahārājairmahābhāgaiḥ prayojyaṃ vyastajātibhiḥ || 47-5 ||

p. 446) tasya nikhiladuḥkhanivartanapūrvakamīpsitaphalasādhanatvam

ādhyātmikādiduḥkhānāṃ trayāṇāmapi nāśanam |
ādhīnāṃ cāpyaśeṣāṇāṃ nāśanaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam || 47-6 ||

sarvārināśanaṃ śāntaṃ mahāvijayakāraṇam |
rakṣohaṇaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ sarvavaśyakaraṃ mune || 47-7 ||

paramāyuḥpradaṃ puṇyaṃ pūrvairnṛpatibhiḥ kṛtam |

ambarīṣaprabhṛtīnāmetadanuṣṭhānāt yathoktaphalalābhaḥ

ambarīṣaḥ śuko'larko māṃdhātā ca purūravāḥ || 47-8 ||

rājoparicaro dhundhuḥ śibiśca śrutakīrtanaḥ |
kṛtvaitaccakravartitvaṃ purā prāpuramī nṛpāḥ || 47-9 ||

nirāmayā niḥsapatnā vistīrṇāmalakīrtayaḥ |

tatra rājñāmadhikāranigamanam

ato mānuṣamānandaṃ kāṅkṣanto bhuvi bhūbhujaḥ || 47-10 ||

kuryuretanmahāśāntilakṣaṇaṃ karma sādaram |

deśaviśeṣavidhiḥ

purād bahirmahānadyāstaṭe devālaye'thavā || 47-11 ||

asaṃbādhe śive deśe vivikte sajalāśaye |
pūrvoktalakṣaṇayutānmaṇḍapādadhikaṃ mune || 47-12 ||

kuryādāyāmavistārau tathā rājāsanānvitam |
p. 447) tattatsamantataḥ kuryādevaṃlakṣaṇamuttamam || 47-13 ||

maṇḍapanirmāṇakramaḥ

bahustambhasamākīrṇaṃ maṇikāñcanamaṇḍitam |
tuṅgatoraṇasaṃyuktaṃ dīpikāśataśobhitam || 47-14 ||

kiṅkiṇījālasaṃyuktaṃ patākābhiralaṃkṛtam |
savitānaṃ sasopānaṃ sarvālaṃkārasaṃyutam || 47-15 ||

candanāgurukarpūradhūpamālādhivāsitam |
tuṅgasālasamāyuktaṃ homaśālābhiveṣṭitam || 47-16 ||

kuṇḍakalpanākramaḥ

evaṃ manojña kṛtvaitat tatra kuṇḍāni kalpayet |
prāgbhāge maṇḍapasyāsya caturdikṣu prakalpayet || 47-17 ||

caturvyūhābhiyogārthaṃ caturaśrāṇi kārayet |
prācyādyānyatha kuṇḍāni keśavādīnatho yajet || 47-18 ||

caturṇāṃ keśavādīnāṃ vikoṇeṣu prakalpayet |
kuṇḍāni śeṣamūrtīnāṃ tacchidreṣu yathākramam || 47-19 ||

tryaśraṃ ṣaḍaśramardhenduṃ yoniṃ padmākṛtiṃ kramāt |
vṛttaṃ padmābhamaṣṭāśraṃ vajrākṛti dhanuḥprabham || 47-20 ||

śūlābhaṃ śūrpakalpaṃ ca kuṇḍānyetāni kārayet |

p. 448) madhye devaṃ pratiṣṭhāpya yantrarūpadharaṃ prabhum || 47-21 ||

vimānalakṣaṇopetaṃ kuṇḍaṃ kuryāt tadagrataḥ |

astrakuṇḍakalpanā

samantāddhomaśālāyāmastrakuṇḍānyanukramāt || 47-22 ||

śataṃ tathādhikaṃ kuryāccatvāriṃśadatandritaḥ |
yathārhamastraliṅgāni maṇḍitānyatha kārayet || 47-23 ||

ṛtvigvaraṇam

antarmaṇḍapakuṇḍeṣu ṛtvijaḥ ṣoḍaśa kramāt |
ṛgbhiḥ puruṣasūktābhirvṛṇuyādvaiṣṇavān sataḥ || 47-24 ||

astrakuṇḍeṣu ṛtvigantaravaraṇam

tataḥ svanāmabhiścānte vṛṇuyādvedapāragān |
bāhyāstrakuṇḍaśālāyāṃ brahmāstrapramukheṣvapi || 47-25 ||

ṛtvijo vṛṇuyāt sarvān kuśalān śāntikarmaṇi |
brahmajajñānamityādi-ṛgbhiḥ pañcāśataṃ kramāt || 47-26 ||

svanāmabhiśca viduṣo brahmāstrādikṛtādikān |
triśataṃ vṛṇuyād brahmasūktaiḥ sadbhiranukramāt || 47-27 ||

tatraivātha bhavā mitra iti pañcabhiranvitaḥ |
p. 449) paro mātrādibhir-ṛgbhistrayodaśabhireva ca || 47-28 ||

viṣṇusūktagatābhiśca asya vāmeti pañcabhiḥ |
ato deveti ṣaḍbhiśca daśabhiścakṣurityapi || 47-29 ||

ṣaḍbhiḥ prati va eneti vṛṇuyāt svasvanāmabhiḥ |
sauvarṇān rājatān vātha kuryāddhomaparicchadān || 47-30 ||

kumbhasthāpanam

pūrvabhāgeṣu kuṇḍānāṃ sthāpayet toyapūritān |
sauvarṇān rājatān kumbhān yadvā tāmramayāniha || 47-31 ||

kumbhān pūrvavadekaikaṃ kalamān bhārasaṃmitān |

homārthamṛtvigantaravaraṇavidhiḥ

vṛṇuyādṛtvijaḥ śiṣṭān yajvano homakarmasu || 47-32 ||

pravartayet svaiḥ svaistu mantrairmantraviduttamaḥ |

madhyamakuṇḍe ācāryakartṛkahomavidhiḥ

deśiko madhyame kuṇḍe sāmadhvanisamanvitam || 47-33 ||

hunedvāditrasaṃyuktaṃ puṇyāhaninadairyutam |

homasaṃkhyāvidhiḥ

pratyekaṃ koṭihomena śāntikarma samārabhet || 47-34 ||

p. 450) 		tattatkumbheṣu tattaddevatābhyarcanavidhi.ḥ

tattaddevān samāhūya kumbheṣu ca pṛthak pṛthak |
svaiḥ svairabhyarcayenmantrairdhūpadīpādibhistathā || 47-35 ||

homānte kumbhodakena rājño'bhiṣekaḥ

homakarmaṇi nirvṛtte tattatkumbhodakaistataḥ |
yantrapīṭhe samāropya rājānamabhiṣecayet || 47-36 ||

astrakuṇḍeṣu homavidhiḥ

astramantreṣu yeṣveva niyuktā ye dvijātayaḥ |
tattatkuṇḍeṣu tairmantrairjuhuyuḥ śāntamānasāḥ || 47-37 ||

uttamamadhyamādhamabhedena homasaṃkhyāvikalpaḥ

pratyekaṃ koṭihomastu mukhyastasyārdhamadhyamaḥ |
adhamaṃ niyutaṃ jñeyaṃ yāvadetat samāpyate || 47-38 ||

kālena tāvatā kāryamapramattairatandritaiḥ |

nārāyaṇīyādyastravarge homasādhanadravyavidhiḥ

nārāyaṇīyaṃ brāhmaṃ ca vimalaṃ krauñcameva ca || 47-39 ||

dharmapāśaṃ ca lakṣākṣaṃ saṃvartaṃ satyamuttamam |
dundunābhaṃ ca śīteṣuṃ viṣṇucakraṃ ca mānavam || 47-40 ||

aindraṃ jyautiṣamityetamastravargaṃ ghṛtairhunet |

p. 451)		kālacakrādivarge caruvidhiḥ

kālacakraṃ dharmacakraṃ vajrāstraṃ ca gadāhvayam || 47-41 ||

brāhmaṃ śirastathaiṣīkaṃ triśūlamaśanidvayam |
kālapāśaṃ ca painākaṃ pāśaṃ vāruṇameva ca || 47-42 ||

āgneyaṃ dayitaṃ cāpi tathā pāśupataṃ param |
eṣa vargastu hotavyaścaruṇaiva yathāvidhi || 47-43 ||

kaṅkālādivarge dadhividhiḥ

kaṅkālaṃ musalaṃ cāpi vāyavyamatha kaṅkaṇī |
asiratnaṃ ca kāpālaṃ śaktirvaidyādharaṃ tathā || 47-44 ||

prasvāpanapraśamane darpaṇaṃ śoṣaṇaṃ tathā |
saṃtāpanaṃ ca gāndharvaṃ paiśācaṃ ca vilāpanam || 47-45 ||

etāni dadhnā hotavyānyastrāṇi paramāstikaiḥ |

madanādivarge payovidhiḥ

madanaṃ tāmasaṃ caiva kandarpadayitaṃ param || 47-46 ||

saumanaṃ musalaṃ caiva saṃvartaṃ ghorameva ca |
māyādharaṃ ca somāstraṃ vāruṇaṃ tvāṣṭrameva ca || 47-47 ||

prasvāpanaṃ bhagāstraṃ ca raudrasṛṣṭaṃ parāṅmukham |
payasāmūni juhuyustadastradhyānatatparāḥ || 47-48 ||

p. 452) 		rabhasādivarge pāyasavidhiḥ

rabhasaṃ satyakīrtiṃ ca pratihārataraṃ tathā |
avāṅmukhaṃ ca viṣamaṃ dṛḍhanābhaṃ sunābhakam || 47-49 ||

daśākṣaṃ śatavaktraṃ ca śataśīrṣaṃ śatodaram |
padmanābhaṃ mahānābhaṃ nairāśyakrathane api || 47-50 ||

vargo'yaṃ pāyasenaiva hotavyaḥ śuddhamānasaiḥ |

yogaṃdharādivarge tilavidhiḥ

yogaṃdharaṃ vinidraṃ ca naidraṃ saumanasaṃ tathā || 47-51 ||

dhṛtimālī pramathanaṃ vidhūtaṃ vṛttimānapi |
mahārcirmālī makaraṃ ruciraṃ karavīrakam || 47-52 ||

amīṣāṃ ca kṛte kāryastilairhomo yathāvidhi |

dhanādivarge madhuvidhiḥ

dhanadhānye kāmarūpaṃ mohaḥ kāmarucistathā || 47-53 ||

varaṇāvaraṇe caiva paramāstre ubhe api |
jṛmbhakaṃ sarvanābhaṃ ca bhṛśāśvatanayo mahān || 47-54 ||

saṃdānaṃ paraśuṃ caiva prīṇīyānmadhunā bhṛśam |

anyeṣvastreṣu śālitaṇḍulavidhiḥ

aparāṇyapyanuktāni juhuyācchālitaṇḍulaiḥ || 47-55 ||

p. 453)	homānte mantraviśeṣeṇa sudarśanatarpaṇavidhiḥ

hutvā yathoktaṃ svairmantraiḥ sarvāstraprabhavaṃ vibhum |
sudarśanākṛtiṃ viṣṇuṃ sarvayantramayaṃ vibhum || 47-56 ||

ṛgbhiryantraniyuktābhiḥ śāntikarma samārabhet |

nārāyaṇīyāditarpaṇe mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

nārāyaṇīyamukhyāstrajātaṃ pratyekamādarāt || 47-57 ||

śāntiṃ kuryāt paro mātretyetatsūktena sādaram |

kālacakrādivarge mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

kālacakrādivargasthaṃ pratyekaṃ jātavedasam || 47-58 ||

prīṇīyāccaiva kaṅkālamukhyavargasya śāntaye |
divasparīti sūktena juhuyājjvalite'nale || 47-59 ||

madanādivarge mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

madanādyastravargasya śāntiṃ kuryāt samāhitaḥ |
ato deveti sūktena tathā viṣṇornukaṃ tviti || 47-60 ||

rabhasādivarge mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

rabhasādyastrajātāni bhavā mitreti pañcabhiḥ |
pratyekaṃ śamayedṛgbhiriti vaidikaśāsanam || 47-61 ||

p. 554)		yogaṃdharādivarge mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

yogaṃdharāderastrasya śāntiḥ kāryā samāhitaiḥ | 
sūktenānena caikaikamato devā avantviti || 47-62 ||

dhanādivarge mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

dhanadhānyādivargasthaṃ mantrajātaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
juṣasva neti sūktena śamayet parameṇa tat || 47-63 ||

anyeṣvastreṣu mantraviśeṣavidhiḥ

astrāṇyanyānyanuktāni viṣṇusūktena tarpayet | 
sūktairetairmahāyantraviniyuktaiśca pāvanaiḥ || 47-64 ||

sarvayantramayaṃ devaṃ stutihomaiḥ samarcayet |

tarpaṇānte sahasrabrāhmaṇabhojanavidhiḥ

tato devān samabhyarcya brāhmaṇāṃstadanantaram || 47-65 ||

sahasraṃ bhojayecchuddhairodanaiḥ pāyasairapi |

tato'stramantrajalaiḥ rājño'bhiṣekavidhiḥ

tato rājāsanārūḍhaṃ rājānaṃ śāntamānasam || 47-66 ||

ṛtvigbhirāhṛtaiḥ sarvairastramantrajalaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
gurustanmūlamantreṇa mantritairabhiṣecayet || 47-67 || 

p. 455)	rugṇasya tu rājñaḥ prokṣaṇavidhiḥ

prokṣayedāmayāviṣṭo yadi rājā bhavenmune |

maṇḍapāntaḥsthakumbhatoyābhiṣecanāvaśyakatā

avaśyaṃ maṇḍapāntaḥsthakumbhatoyābhiṣecanam |
kuryānnirāmayo'nādhirvijayī bhavati kṣaṇāt || 47-68 ||

yathoktamahāśāntiphalakīrtanam

ārogyamāyurvijayo vibhūti-
rvaśitvamāstikyamamartyatā ca |
sarvārthasiddhirvasudhādhipatyaṃ
bhavanti niḥsaṃśayameva rājñaḥ || 47-69 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
mahāśāntividhānaṃ nāma saptacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 47 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 2936



śrīsudarśanamahāyantraghaṭitāsanādiprabhāvavarṇanaṃ 
nāmāṣṭācatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

iṣṭārthasādhane japādivyatiriktopāyapraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

kathitaṃ bhagavan sarvaṃ japahomārcanādikam |
abhiṣeko mahāśāntirmahāśāntikriyāvidhiḥ || 48-1 ||

sarvārthasādhakatvena cakrarūpasya śārṅgiṇaḥ |
etairvinā kimiṣṭārthasādhanaṃ vidyate na vā || 48-2 ||

tatrāsanādīnāmupāyatvakathanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

vinaitānyapyabhīṣṭārthamalabhanta pare nṛpāḥ |
āsanenāṅgulīyena darpaṇena dhvajena ca || 48-3 ||

vitānena ca vistīrṇabhūtayo devasaṃnibhāḥ |
muktāpīḍo viśālaśca sunandaścitraśekharaḥ || 48-4 ||

p. 457) kīirtimālī ca bhūpālāḥ pālayanto'khilāṃ bhuvam |

āsanādibhirmuktāpīḍādīnāmiṣṭasiddhiḥ

āsanenaiva vasudhāṃ muktāpīḍo'jayat purā || 48-5 ||

viśālo'pyajayanmṛtyumaṅgulīyena bhūpatiḥ |
sunando darpaṇenaiva nāgalokaṃ mahīpatiḥ || 48-6 ||

dhvajenāsurasaṃgrāmamajayaccitraśekharaḥ |
kīrtimālī vitānena tridivaṃ prāpa bhūpatiḥ || 48-7 ||

kevalāsanādīnāmiṣṭasādhanatvakathaṃtāpraśnaḥ

nāradaḥ �

kevalānyāsanādīni sādhanatvaṃ kathaṃ yayuḥ |
sādhyaṃ prati vibho tanme kathayedaṃ mahādbhutam || 48-8 ||

āsanaprabhāvavarṇanāya muktāpīḍopākhyānakathanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

muktāpīḍo mahātejā rājā suśravasaḥ sutaḥ |
pitaryuparate pṛthvīṃ bubhuje sāgarāmbarām || 48-9 ||

hārāpīḍasya viṣayāsaktatayā rājye dharmavicyutiḥ

nijāntaḥpurayoṣitsu viṣayāsaktamānasaḥ |
anālocitasaptāṅgo rājā rājīvalocanaḥ || 48-10 ||

p. 458) prajāstadviṣaye tena dharmavicyutimācaran |
balibhirdasyubhī rājyaṃ kṣubhitaṃ ca hṛtaṃ ca tat || 48-11 ||

tasmai prakṛtayaḥ sarvā rājñe kṣobhaṃ vyajijñapan |
bubudhe na sa tenāpi madhumaithunavihvalaḥ || 48-12 ||

sapurodhasāmamātyānāṃ mantraṇaprakāraḥ

tataḥ purodhasā sārdhamamātyāḥ saṃnyamantrayan |
rājā tu viṣayāsakto rājyaṃ dasyubhirāplutam || 48-13 ||

iti saṃcintya te sarve guruṃ prapya mahādyutim |
bhavatprasādājjeṣyāmo nānyā kāpyasti no gatiḥ || 48-14 ||

ityuktastaiḥ purodhāstu tathaivetyāha tān prati | 

purodhasā vasiṣṭhopadiṣṭopāyānuṣṭhānam

ityuktvā yantraghaṭitaṃ viṣṭaraṃ ratnamaṇḍitam || 48-15 ||

vasiṣṭhoktena mārgeṇa cakārāsmai mahādbhutam |
tasminnāropayāmāsa rājānamabalāratam || 48-16 ||

purodhasā rājānaṃ prati kṛtyaviśeṣānuśāsanam

p. 459) āśīrmaṅgalayuktāni gītavāditranartakān || 48-17 ||

kalaśodakamakṣayyamamṛtaṃ bhāvayed budhaḥ |
tanmadhyādutthite padme sauvarṇe cāṣṭapatrake || 48-18 ||

caturbāhuṃ trinetraṃ ca padyahastaṃ hiraṇmayam |
sarvābharaṇasaṃchannaṃ muktāhāravirājitam || 48-19 ||

kirīṭahārakeyūrakaṭakādivibhūṣitam |
pītāmbaradharaṃ saumyaṃ prasannavadanaṃ śubham || 48-20 ||

kaṭisūtrasamāyuktaṃ kuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam |
śrīvatsavakṣasaṃ śrīśaṃ śrīkaraṃ śrīniketanam || 48-21 ||

śriyā juṣṭaṃ sadā samyaktāmrapadmanibhekṣaṇam |
padmanābhamudārāṅgaṃ bhaktārtiśamanaṃ harim || 48-22 ||

tasyāṅke dakṣiṇe lakṣmīṃ samāsīnāṃ vicintayet |
mantramūrtyātmako devo mantravācyastu sa prabhuḥ || 48-23 ||

praviśantaṃ tatastaṃ tu bhāvayenmantrarūpiṇam |
evaṃ saṃcintayan mantraṃ japet kalaśamāmṛśan || 48-24 ||

aṣṭottarāyutaṃ japtvā abhiṣiñcan svakāṃ tanum |
grahāṇāṃ navakānāṃ ca sadyaḥ śāntikaro bhavet || 48-25 ||

p. 460) āyuḥ śriyaṃ ca yasyecchet taṃ ca tenābhiṣecayet |
arcayedṛtvijaḥ sarvān pūrvameva budho naraḥ || 48-26 ||

brāhmaṇāṃstarpayeccaiva dakṣiṇāmuttamāṃ dadat |
evaṃ svasyāparasyāpi hitaṃ kuryāt tathātmanaḥ || 48-27 ||

dakṣiṇā cātra deyā syāt suvarṇaṃ śatameva vā |
keśān dhunenna na vapenna spṛśet snānaśāṭikām || 48-28 ||

nātiṣṭhet snānasalilamalaṃkuryāt tato budhaḥ |
maṅgalāni ca paśyettu ṛṣabhaṃ maṅgalāḥ striyaḥ || 48-29 ||

ātmano janmanakṣatre śuklavāsā bhavet tathā |
śukravāreṣu kurvīta saptaśaḥ kurvatastathā || 48-30 ||

kāmāḥ saṃkalpitāstasya dhruvaṃ sidhyanti nānyathā |
udvahan vāri kurvīta śrīkāmaḥ pāpanāśanam || 48-31 ||

tiṣṭhannañjalibhiḥ prātaraṣṭottaraśataṃ punaḥ |
abhimantrya jalaṃ mūrdhni kṣipet pāpapraṇāśanam || 48-32 ||

juhuyādvastrakāmastu kusumaiḥ kumudādibhiḥ |
yadvarṇaṃ juhuyāt puṣpaṃ tadvarṇaṃ vastramāpnuyāt || 48-33 ||

vṛṣṭikāmaḥ śucau deśe gomayenopalepite |
raktadravyeṇa tatraiva maṇḍalaṃ parikalpayet || 48-34 ||

maṇḍalasya purastāttu navakumbhaṃ nidhāpayet |
p. 461) tantunā veṣṭitaṃ samyak pūritaṃ gandhavāriṇā || 48-35 ||

āvāhayet tataḥ sūryaṃ kumbhe śubhre samāhitaḥ |
tatrasthamarcayed viṣṇuṃ pūrvavat sādhakottamaḥ || 48-36 ||

pāyasaṃ juhuyāt tatra śatamaṣṭottaraṃ budhaḥ |
dhyāyannekāgradhīḥ siṃhaṃ japenmantraṃ sahasraśaḥ || 48-37 ||

evaṃ kurvīta saptāhaṃ dvādaśāhamathāpi vā |
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān mahāvṛṣṭiṃ vimuñcati || 48-38 ||

vaitasenaiva homena vṛṣṭirbhavati sarvathā |
anaśnannārdravāsasko vṛṣṭikāmaḥ sadā japet || 48-39 ||

saptāhājjāyate vṛṣṭirmahatī divyaśāsanāt |
bhasmahomena vā samyag lavaṇenāthavā punaḥ || 48-40 ||

vṛṣṭiṃ nivārayed bhūyaḥ śāntiḥ kāryā manīṣiṇā |
yastu mantraṃ japennityaṃ prātaḥ snātvā sahasraśaḥ || 48-41 ||

śrīrārogyaṃ ca tejaśca vardhate tasya sarvadā |
sarvāhaḥsu japedyastu kṣīrāhāro jitendriyaḥ || 48-42 ||

arcanaṃ ca yathāśakti kurvanniyutamanvaham |
saṃsiddhimatulāṃ gacchedaṇimādiguṇairyutām || 48-43 ||

ahorātroṣito yastu pañcagavyaṃ pibet tataḥ |
nityamaṣṭasahasraṃ tu sarvapāpapraṇāśanam || 48-44 ||

p. 462) pañcagavyaṃ tu saṃpītvā japet pañca dināni vai |
brahmahatyādibhiḥ sadyo mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 48-45 ||

vāpayet sarvabījāni hyabhimantrya sahasrakam |
mahatī sasyasaṃpattirjāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 48-46 ||

ghṛtena pāyasā cāpi snāpayed devamuttamam |
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān dadāti manasepsitam || 48-47 ||

aśnīyādanvahaṃ vidvānannaṃ saptābhimantritam |
parakarmācarannasminnāsīnaḥ kuru sarvadā || 48-48 ||

yathopadiṣṭānuṣṭhānena rājño niṣkaṇṭakabhūmilābhaḥ

tenaivamanuśiṣṭo'yaṃ māsamātramathākarot |
tenāsya śatravaḥ sarve vinaṣṭā vyādhibhirbalaiḥ || 48-49 ||

tena niṣkaṇṭakā bhūmirvaśyābhūt tasya sārṇavā |

aṅgulīyaprabhāvavarṇanāya viśālopākhyānārambhaḥ

viśālo nāma rājāyaṃ viśālākhye pure vasan || 48-50 ||

pālayāmāsa dharmātmā medinīṃ sābdhipattanām |

viśālaguṇavarṇanam

vasan viṃśatime varṣe brāhmaṇānāmupāsakaḥ |

p. 463) prajānurañjako dhīro daive pitrye ca niṣṭhitaḥ || 48-52 ||

tajjananyāḥ svaputramaraṇasūcakākāśavāṇīśravaṇādvilāpaḥ

rājñā mātuḥ samāśvāsanapūrvakaṃ purodhasaḥ

p. 464) 	rājñā yathopadiṣṭānuṣṭhānam

iti śiṣṭo'ṅgulīyaṃ sa āhūyainamadhārayat || 48-59 ||

prāṇānādātumājagmustasyāntikamavāritāḥ |

p. 465) 	sumatināmnastatputrasya nāgakanyayā nāgaloke nayanam

lebhe cireṇa tanayamayamiṣṭvā śatakratum |
cakāra sumatiṃ nāmnā vardhamānaṃ dine dine || 48-66 ||

janakastaṃ tataḥ śrīmān kālena prāptayauvanaḥ |
so'bhūdanalpakālena prāptavidyo'strakovidaḥ || 48-67 ||

ekadā mṛgayāṃ kartuṃ bāhyopavanamabhyagāt |
tatrāpaśyanmanojñāṅgīṃ rūpayauvanaśālinīm || 48-68 ||

adṛṣṭapūrvāṃ tāṃ dṛṣṭvā mumoha madanāturaḥ |
sumatiṃ taṃ samādāya sā jagāma svamālayam || 48-69 ||

māyayā mohayitvainaṃ nāgalokaṃ viveśa sā |

tasyānaṅgamañjarīṃ vāsukitanayāṃ pariṇīya tatraiva cirakālanivāsaḥ

tasmin bhogavatīṃ ramyāṃ nāgarājapurīṃ tadā || 48-70 ||

praviśyānaṅgamañjaryai prāyacchat sumatiṃ ramā |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā paramaprītā sā vavre sumatiṃ patim || 48-71 ||

taṃ lokaṃ tāṃ purīṃ tāṃ ca nāgakanyāṃ manoharām |
vilokya vismayaṃ prāpa sa tāṃ cānaṅgamañjarīm || 48-72 ||

gāndharveṇa vivāhena parigṛhya tayā saha |
reme vismṛtasarvasvapurīpitṛparigrahaḥ || 48-73 ||

 p. 466) 	putravirahāt khidyatā rājñā cāraiḥ putrānveṣaṇam

etasminnantare rājā śuśrāvānāgataṃ sutam |

vidikṣu dikṣu koṇeṣu giriṣvapsu saritsu ca |

pattaneṣvapyaraṇyeṣu tīrtheṣvanviṣya sarvataḥ |

svaguruṃ kaṇvamāsādya tamasātīravāsinam || 48-80 ||

vṛttaṃ vijñāpayāmāsa rājaputrasya tādṛśam |

p. 467) 	kaṇvamuninā rājakumāravṛttāntasyāviṣkaraṇam

tacchrutvā sa muniryukto babhūva kṛpayā punaḥ || 48-81 ||

rūpalāvaṇyasaṃpannā kālena prāptayauvanā || 48-84 ||

asyā yasmin vare lipsā tasmai dāsyāmi tāmiti |
saṃkalpaṃ vāsukermatvā ceṣṭyaśceruritastataḥ || 48-85 ||

tasyāḥ sadṛśamanveṣṭuṃ varaṃ taṃ naiva lebhire |
nāgaloke tathā bhūmyāṃ vyacaran varavāñchayā || 48-86 ||

tatrainaṃ sumatiṃ dṛṣṭvā rājaputraṃ manoharam |
procustasyā mahārājatanayo madanopamaḥ || 48-87 ||

tavaiva sadṛśo bhūmyāṃ puruhūtasamaḥ śriyā |
na ca sāmādibhiḥ śakyaḥ samānetumasāviti || 48-88 ||

tadākarṇya tamatraiva kṣipramānayateti tāḥ |
uktvā tatsaṃgamautsukyāt parāṃ cintāmupāgatā || 48-89 ||

tataḥ saṃmantrya tāḥ sakhyo ramāṃ māyāviśāradām |
p. 468) māyayā mohayitvainamihānaya manoramam || 48-90 ||

ityūcuḥ sā tathoktainamānayāmāsa māyayā |
sumatistāmathodvāhya ramate tatra sāṃpratam || 48-91 ||

kumārapratyāharaṇe sudarśanayantraghaṭitadarpaṇasyaiva 
sādhanatvopadeśaḥ

sudarśanaprabhāveṇa vināharaṇakarmaṇi |
nāstyupāyāntaraṃ tasya tatastenāharāmyaham || 48-92 ||

sudarśanamahāyantraṃ darpaṇīkṛtya hāṭakaiḥ |
pūrvoktavartmanā tena tamānayata mā ciram || 48-93 ||

ityuktvā sa punaścakre tapaḥ paramadāruṇam |

yathopadiṣṭānuṣṭhānāt rājño nāgalokagamanasiddhiḥ

purohito viniṣkramya rājñe sarvaṃ nyavedayat || 48-94 ||

rājā ca paramaprītaḥ kṛtvā yantritadarpaṇam |
rathe purastāt saṃsthāpya biladvāraṃ tadā yayau || 48-95 ||

tenābhigantuṃ śakto'bhūt sunando darpaṇaujasā |

atyadbhutanāgalokadarśanāt rājño vismayātiśayaḥ

praviśya lokaṃ nāgānāṃ svargalokamivāparam || 48-96 ||

vismayaṃ paramaṃ prāpa tatsamṛddhivilokanāt |

rājñā sadārasya kumārasya svapurīṃ prati pratyānayanam

tasmin bhogavatīṃ ramyāṃ nāgarājapurīmasau || 48-97 ||

p. 469) praviśyāthānayāmāsa putraṃ paramavatsalaḥ |
sadāraḥ pitaraṃ draṣṭuṃ śuddhāntāt sumatistadā || 48-98 ||

niryayau vanitālokasahito madano yathā |
taṃ tābhirnāgakanyābhiḥ saha syandanamuttamam || 48-99 ||

āropya svapurīṃ rājā pratasthe paravīrahā |

tataḥ kruddhasya vāsukeryuddhasamudyogaḥ

tava jāmātaraṃ putrīṃ svāvarodhānmahīpatiḥ || 48-100 ||

kaścit syandanamāropya yātītyākarṇya vāsukiḥ |
niryayau paramakruddhaḥ phaṇisenāparīvṛtaḥ || 48-101 ||

sa dṛṣṭvā rājaśārdūlaṃ tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti cābravīt |
kva gamyate samādāya vadhūṃ varamitastvayā || 48-102 ||

sudarśanayantraghaṭitadarpaṇaprabhāvāt rājño vijayalābhaḥ

sunando'tha samākarṇya nivartya syandanottamam |
vavre darpaṇamadyaiva śatrusenāṃ jahīti vai || 48-103 ||

tataḥ prasvāpanāgneye divyāstre niḥsṛte kṣaṇāt |
prasvāpanaṃ nāgasenāṃ viveśānena sāsvapat || 48-104 ||

āgneyamastramārabdha tāṃ purīṃ dagdhumojasā | 

parājitena vāsukinā śaraṇāgatipūrvakaṃ rājābhyarhaṇam

tad dṛṣṭvā nāgarājastaṃ śaraṇaṃ samupāgamat || 48-105 ||

p. 470) nājñāyi sarvamasmābhistadetat kṣamyatāṃ vibho |
atyāhitamidaṃ strīṇāmiti matvā śamaṃ vraja || 48-106 ||

gṛhāṇemāni ratnāni mahārhāṇi mahādyute |
imāṃ ca kanyāmanyāśca sahasraṃ nāgayoṣitaḥ || 48-107 ||

ādāya pratisaṃhṛtya divyāstre gantumarhasi |

tato rājñaḥ saparikarasya svapurapraveśaḥ

sunando'pi tathetyuktvā saṃhṛtyāstre samāhitaḥ || 48-108 ||

tataḥ svaputraṃ ratnāni mahānti varayoṣitaḥ |
ādāyāmantrya taṃ rājā jagāma svapurīṃ kṛtī || 48-109 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
śrīsudarśanamahāyantraghaṭitāsanādiprabhāvavarṇanaṃ nāma 
aṣṭācatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ || 48 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 3045



sudarśanamahāyantraghaṭitadhvajaprabhāvanirūpaṇaṃ nāma 
ekonapañcāśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

sudarśanayantraghaṭitadhvajaprabhāvanirūpaṇapratijñā

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

sudarśanamahāyantraghaṭitasya dhvajasya tu |
prabhāvaḥ śrūyatāṃ samyag yāthātathyena nārada || 49-1 ||

citraśekharādhiṣṭhitabhadravāṭīnagaravarṇanam

sarasvatyāstaṭe ramye bhadravāṭīti viśrutā |
paraḥsahasraiḥ prāsādairjātarūpamayaiścitā || 49-2 ||

purī vividhakāmāḍhyā hemasālasamanvitā |
samṛddhyā tridivaprakhyā babhūva bhuvi durlabhā || 49-3 ||

tāmadhyāste mahāvīryo rājoparicarātmajaḥ |
pālayannaikavarṣāṇi citraśekharasaṃjñitaḥ || 49-4 ||

p. 472) tatpitroparicareṇa kadācid dvandvayuddhe 
śaṅkukarṇākhyāsurahananam

pitaryasya purā divyamindradattaṃ kramāgatam |
rathamāruhya devānāṃ kāryārthaṃ yāti khe balī || 49-5 ||

śaṅkukarṇa iti khyāto dānavo bhīmalocanaḥ |
jighṛkṣustadrathaṃ mārge tasya kūbaramagrahīt || 49-6 ||

uvāca ca mayā sārdhaṃ yudhyasva yadi vā ratham |
jahi vetyuktavatyasmin sa tathetyagrahīd dhanuḥ || 49-7 ||

tatastena mahāyuddhe dinamekaṃ pravartite |
athāstrajālamutsṛjya dvandvayuddhe hato'suraḥ || 49-8 ||

amarṣaṇanāmnā śaṅkukarṇasutena citraśekharapīḍanam

jitvā tamasuraṃ rājñi kālenāstaṃ gate mune |
tatputtraṃ śaṅkukarṇasya sutaḥ paramaroṣaṇaḥ || 49-9 ||

amarṣaṇa iti khyāto māyāvī citraśekharam |
jighāṃsustadbalaṃ rājñaḥ puraṃ cāpīḍayat sadā || 49-10 ||

ekadāmarṣaṇaḥ kruddho bhadravāṭīmupāgamat |
pitṛhantṛsutaṃ yoddhuṃ sasainyaṃ citraśekharam || 49-11 ||

nihatya cainamādāsye sarvaṃ tatsyandanottamam |
ityuktvā nagarīṃ ramyāṃ rurodha baladarpitaḥ || 49-12 ||

p. 473) 	citraśekharasya tena saha ghorayuddham

niryayau mahatīṃ senāṃ saṃnahya nṛpatiḥ purāt |
tatra yuddhaṃ samabhavadrājñastena durātmanā || 49-13 ||

saraṇaiścābhisaraṇaiḥ kacākaci bhayāvaham |
gajāśvarathapādātaṃ prabhagnarathamākulam || 49-14 ||

pravṛttāsṛksaritsārthaṃ kṛttacchatradhvajācitam |
nṛtyatkabandhamutkṛttadaṣṭādharaśiraścitam || 49-15 ||

dinatrayaṃ yudhyamānasyāpi vijayālābhaḥ

evaṃ dinatrayaṃ yuddhaṃ pravṛttaṃ romaharṣaṇam |
na lebhe vijayaṃ tasminnāhave citraśekharaḥ || 49-16 ||

śrāntasenayordvayorapi yuddhāt pratinivṛttiḥ

śramānnivavṛte senādvayī tasmād dvayorapi |
tāvubhau jagmatuḥ svaṃ svaṃ puraṃ viśrāntavāhanau || 49-17 ||

evaṃ saptadaśavāraṃ yudhyamānasyāpi vijayāsiddhiḥ

vārān saptadaśaitena yuyudhe citraśekharaḥ |
evameva jayaṃ prāpa na kvacit pṛthivīpatiḥ || 49-18 ||

khinnasya rājñaḥ cintāprakāraḥ

atha durjayamālocya mahāsuramamarṣaṇam |

p. 474) kenopāyena jetavya iti cintāparo'bhavat || 49-19 ||

mahādevaprasādena vinā naiva jayo mama |
atastaṃ tapasā devaṃ toṣyāmīti mano dadhe || 49-20 ||

kṛtaniścayasya kailāsagamanam

kṛtī niścitya manasā rājyaṃ saṃnyasya mantriṣu |
jagāma tapase rājā rathamāruhya kāmagam || 49-21 ||

kailāsaśikharaṃ ramyaṃ nabhasā vasudhādhipaḥ |

gagane gacchato'sya mandaropari syandanagatipratirodhaḥ

tenaiva gacchatastasya syandanaṃ mandaropari || 49-22 ||

atiṣṭhat preryamāṇeṣu varāśveṣvapi nārada | 

tatkāraṇajijñāsayā caratastatra kuberadarśanam

rathaḥ kasmādakasmānme viṣṭabdhaḥ iti vismitaḥ || 49-23 ||

rathādavātaracchṛṅge tasya ramye mahīpatiḥ |
avatīrya rathāt tasmāccacāra girimūrdhani || 49-24 ||

tatrāpaśyat sarastīre taruṇaṃ divyalakṣaṇam |
upāsya paścimāṃ saṃdhyāmavasthitamanākulam || 49-25 ||

taruṇādityasaṃkāśaṃ jvalantaṃ prabhayā svayā |

p. 475) 		tena samaṃ saṃlāpaḥ

taṃ praṇamya mahābāhuḥ kṛtāñjalirabhāṣata || 49-26 ||

kastvaṃ purāṇapuruṣo yadi vā kamalodbhavaḥ |
umeśvaro diśeśānāṃ yadi vānyatamo bhavān || 49-27 ||

ityuktastena bhagavān prāha gambhīrayā girā |
kiṃnāmako'si kathaya kimāgamanakāraṇam || 49-28 ||

ekākinā tvayā deśaḥ kathaṃ prāpto'yamityapi |

rājñā kubere svavṛttāntasya nivedanam

atha nyavedayat sarvaṃ rājoparicarātmajaḥ || 49-29 ||

citraśekharanāmānamātmānamavanīpatim |
mahāsurapravṛttiṃ ca svasya tena ca pīḍanam || 49-30 ||

tajjayāyeśatuṣṭyarthaṃ tapaścaraṇakāraṇāt |
syandanenābhigamanaṃ nirodhaṃ tatra tasya tu || 49-31 ||

kuberavacanāt tatra girau lakṣmīnivāsaparijñānam

ityuktastena so'pyāha viddhi māṃ guhyakeśvaram |
atraivāste mahālakṣmīḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam || 49-32 ||

tatsevārthamiha prāptaḥ sā hi sarvagatiḥ parā |
tadājñayaiva carataścandrasūryau vihāyasi || 49-33 ||

tatprabhāveṇa te bhagnaṃ gamanaṃ syandanasya tu |

p. 476)  	lakṣmīprasādanopadeśapūrvakaṃ kuberāntardhānam

tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sādhayābhīṣṭamityuktvāntaradhīyata || 49-34 ||

rājasāhāyyārthaṃ kuberaprahitapuruṣasamāgamaḥ

tasya sāhāyyakāmo'nyastannideśādupāgamat |
praṇamya bhūpatiṃ prāha guhyako'haṃ tadājñayā || 49-35 ||

sāhāyyakāmaste prāpto vāsāvo'traiva śarvarīm |
pratyūṣe'pekṣitaṃ yat syāt tat kariṣyāmi nānyathā || 49-36 ||

tadvacanāt tatraiva sthāne tadrātriyāpanam

parasparapraṇayinau tāṃ rātriṃ tatra coṣya tu |
devyāḥ sadasi gandharvagītavāditraniḥsvanam || 49-37 ||

śṛṇvantau tatkathābhiśca ninyatustāṃ sarastaṭhe |

prabhāte rājñā lakṣmīnivāsasthānajijñāsayā guhyakaṃ prati praśnaḥ

pratyūṣasamaye saṃdhyāmupāsya prathamāṃ nṛpaḥ || 49-38 ||

guhyakaṃ prāha śaile'smin devī sā kutra vartate |
kenādhvanā vā taddhāma yāma taddarśanaṃ kadā || 49-39 || 

guhyakasya prativacanam

guhyakaḥ pratyuvācedaṃ krośe tanmandiraṃ param |
p. 477) yato gītamṛdaṅgānāṃ nisvanaḥ śrūyate mahān || 49-40 ||

kaṃcit kālamupāsyaināṃ dhanado yāsyati kṣaṇāt |
tasmin gate praviśyaināṃ namasyasi sanātanīm || 49-41 ||

tato guhyakena saha lakṣmīmandiragamanam

prasthāpayāmāsa tato yāmamātraṃ pratīkṣya tam |
rājāpi svarathaṃ tatra saṃsthāpyainamathānvagāt || 49-42 ||

guhādvāradeśe guhyakasyāvasthānam

tato mahāguhāpārśve mandiraṃ samadṛśyata |
tadguhādvāri so'tiṣṭhat pālayan bilagahvaram || 49-43 ||

rājño mandirāntaḥstharatnamaṇḍape praveśaḥ

atītya ca tataḥ kakṣyāścatasro maṇigocarāḥ |
tato ratnamayaṃ divyaṃ viveśāsthānamaṇḍapam || 49-44 ||

maṇḍapavarṇanam

maṇisthūṇāsahasrāḍhyaṃ maṇḍapaṃ samadṛśyata | 
paraḥśatamahāratnaparyaṅkasamalaṃkṛtam || 49-45 ||

ratnamaṅgaladīpaiśca maṇḍitaṃ tuṅgatoraṇam |
anekakalpakākīrṇaṃ maṇipīṭhapariṣkṛtam || 49-46 ||

mandārakusumāmodairmṛganābhimadairapi |
p. 478) kastūrikotthasaurabhyairvāsitāntaramūrjitam || 49-47 ||

atyādityaprabhaṃ puṇyaṃ muktādāmavibhūṣitam |
hemapaṅkajamālāḍhyaṃ patākādhvajaśobhitam || 49-48 ||

dhūpaiḥ kālāgarūdbhūtaiḥ samantādadhivāsitam |
karpūramiśrakāleyakṣodapaṅkapariṣkṛtam || 49-49 ||

itastataścarantīnāṃ śaktīnāṃ bhūṣaṇāravaiḥ |
jhaṇajjhaṇitamañjīraninadairnāditāntaram || 49-50 ||

maṇḍapamadhyastharantaparyaṅke abjāsanasthamahālakṣmīdarśanam

tanmadhye ratnaparyaṅke saṃskṛtābjāsanāśritām |
mahālakṣmīṃ mahānandāṃ saṃśritāṃ sarvaśaktibhiḥ || 49-51 ||

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ sarvālaṃkāraśobhitām |
lāvaṇyāmṛtakalpāṅgīṃ śaśibimbanibhānanām || 49-52 ||

saṃvītadivyakauśeyāṃ divyacandanamālikām |
vilokya praṇato bhūtvā vismayastimito'bhavat || 49-53 ||

tatpādodakaprāśanaparipūtena rājñā tatstutikaraṇam

tatpādāmbu samādāya ratirenamapāyayat |
so'tha tuṣṭāva suprītastāṃ devīṃ śubhalocanām || 49-54 ||

p. 479) 	tatra lakṣmyāḥ ubhayavibhūtyaiśvaryapratipādanam

mahālakṣmi bhadre paravyomavāsi-
nyanante suṣumnāhvaye sūrijuṣṭe |
jaye sūrituṣṭe śaraṇye sukīrte
prasādaṃ prapanne mayi tvaṃ kuruṣva || 49-55 ||

sati svasti te gauri gāyatri gārgi
dhruve kāmadheno surādhīśavandye |
sunīte supūrṇenduśīte kumāri
prasādaṃ prapanne mayi tvaṃ kuruṣva || 49-56 ||

sadā siddhagandharvayakṣeśavidyā-
dharaiḥ stūyamāne rame rāmarāme |
praśaste samastāmarīsevyamāne
prasādaṃ prapanne mayi tvaṃ kuruṣva || 49-57 ||

lakṣmyā eva nirvyājapuruṣakāratvasamarthanam

duritaughanivāraṇapravīṇe
vimale bhāsurabhāgadheyalabhye |
praṇavapratipādyavasturūpa-
sphuraṇākhye harivallabhe namaste || 49-58 ||

p. 480) siddhe sādhye mantramūrte vareṇye
gupte tṛpte nityamudgīthavidye |
vyakte vidvadbhāvite bhāvanākhye
bhadre bhadraṃ dehi me saṃśritāya || 49-59 ||

sarvādhāre sadgate'dhyātmavidye
bhāvinyārdre nirvṛte'dhyātmavalli |
viśvādhyakṣe maṅgalāvāsabhūme
bhadre bhadraṃ dehi me saṃśritāya || 49-60 ||

lakṣmyāḥ pāramārthikamaṅgalaguṇākaratayā śrutiśirobhūṣaṇatvam

amoghaseve nijasadguṇaughe
vidīpitānuśravamūrdhabhāge |
ahetumīmāṃsyamahānubhāve
vilokane māṃ viṣayīkuruṣva || 49-61 ||

umāśacīprabhṛtīnāṃ mahālakṣmīvibhūtitvam

umāśacīkīrtisarasvatīdhī-
svāhādinānāvidhaśaktibhede | 
aśeṣalokābharaṇasvarūpe
vilokane māṃ viṣayīkuruṣva || 49-62 ||

p. 481) 	stutiprītayā lakṣmyā rājābhīṣṭasya praśnaḥ

iti stutiṃ niśamyaiṣā prasannā prāha pārthivam |
prārthitaṃ te dadāmyadya kiṃ tat kathaya bhūpate || 49-63 ||

rājñā svavṛttāntasya nivedanam | lakṣmyā 
sudarśanayantraghaṭitadhvajasyārpaṇam

rājā ca svānvayaṃ svaṃ ca mahāsuranipīḍitam |
ācakhyau sarvamevāsyai mā bhaiṣīriti sābravīt || 49-64 ||

ahamādhārabhūtāpi śaktīnāṃ jagatastathā |
sudarśanaprabhāveṇa karomyāśritarakṣaṇam || 49-65 ||

anena jahi te śatrumiti dhvajamathārpayat |

lakṣmīdattadhvajamahimnā śatrujayapūrvakaṃ rājño 
niṣkaṇṭakarājyalābhaḥ

yantritaṃ taṃ dhvajaṃ labdhvā jagāma svapuraṃ nṛpaḥ || 49-66 ||

jigāya tena mahatīmāsurīṃ sa varūthinīm |
taṃ ca hatvā sukhaṃ rājyamakaroccitraśekharaḥ || 49-67 ||

iti śrīpāñcarātre tantrarahasye ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ 
sudarśanamahāyantraghaṭitadhvajaprabhāvanirūpaṇaṃ nāma 
ekonapañcāśo'dhyāyaḥ || 49 ||

āditaḥ ślokāḥ 3112



kīrtimālyupākhyānavarṇanaṃ nāma pañcāśo'dhyāyaḥ

dhyātaṃ sakṛdbhavānekakoṭyaghaughaṃ haratyaram |
sudarśanasya tad divyaṃ bhargo devasya dhīmahi ||

vakṣyamāṇārthasūcanam

ahirbudhnyaḥ �

idaṃ tu śṛṇu devarṣe kīrtimālī vitānataḥ | 
ajayat tridivaṃ pūrvaṃ yenaiva vidhinā balī || 50-1 ||

bhadraśṛṅganāmnā rājñā bhūmaṇḍalasya paripālanam

bhadraśṛṅga iti khyāto rājā paramadhārmikaḥ |
āsīdudāracarito viśālākhye pure purā || 50-2 ||

tasya svapuravat pṛthvīṃ saptadvīpavatīmapi |
śāsataḥ śakrakalpasya śaradāmayutaṃ gatam || 50-3 ||

tatputrasya kīrtimālino guṇavarṇanam

tasya dīrghāyuṣaḥ putraḥ kīrtimālī madoddhataḥ |
śūraḥ kamalapatrākṣaḥ kandarpa iva rūpavān || 50-4 ||

p. 483) balavānapratidvandvaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ |
vinayajño mahodāraḥ pratāpī satyasaṃgaraḥ || 50-5 ||

samareṣvaparāvartī gambhīro dhanadarpitaḥ |
meghagambhīranirhrādī jigīṣuḥ śatrutāpanaḥ || 50-6 ||

mattadvipa iva svairaṃ cacāra jagatīmimām |

pituranantaraṃ tenāpi dharmato rājyasya pālanam

tasyaivaṃ vartamānasya pitā mṛtyumupāgataḥ || 50-7 ||

pitaryuparate śrīmāñchaśāsa pitṛvad bhuvam |
jagatyāḥ sarvavṛttāntaṃ cāraiḥ kaiścidviveda saḥ || 50-8 ||

svapure svayamaṅgeṣu caritaṃ veda saptasu |

kadācit niśīthe gūḍhaṃ saṃcaratastasya purād bahirvipradarśanam

ekākī caikadā dhanvī nṛpaḥ svairakathā nṛṇām || 50-9 ||

śrotuṃ niśīthe nirgatya sarvato vyacarat puram |
āpaṇeṣu ca rathyāsu catvareṣu gṛheṣu ca || 50-10 ||

sarvadvāreṣu vapreṣu sarasīṣu vaneṣu ca |
upakāryāsu caityeṣu devatāyataneṣu ca || 50-11 ||

śastraśālāsu tīrtheṣu vaneṣūdyānabhūmiṣu |
saṃcaran vipramāsīnaṃ śamīmūle purād bahiḥ || 50-12 ||

p. 484) dadarśa rājā nirbhīko jvalantamiva pāvakam |
prasādhitatanuṃ śāntaṃ sākṣātkṛtasudarśanam || 50-13 ||

saṃprāptasarvakāmaṃ taṃ yogārūḍhamakalmaṣam |
praśrayāvanato bhūtvā papracchedaṃ mahīpatiḥ || 50-14 ||

tadvṛttāntajijñāsoḥ rājñastaṃ prati praśnaḥ

kastvaṃ kimarthamadhunā vṛkṣamūlamupāśritaḥ |
kasmādupāgataḥ sarvamācakṣvānagha pṛcchate || 50-15 ||

yogārūḍhasya tasya rājapraśnamajānato maunībhāvaḥ

evamukto'pi yogastho na vedaitadudīritam |
tathā punaḥ punaścokto rājānaṃ nābhyabhāṣata || 50-16 ||

balād grahītumudyuktasya rājño vipraprabhāvāt stambhībhāvaḥ

balād grahītumārebhe brāhmaṇaṃ kupito nṛpaḥ |
tatastasya prabhāveṇa stabdhaceṣṭo'bhavat tadā || 50-17 ||

vismitena rājñā stutibhirviprasya prasādanam

stabdhamātmānamālakṣya vismayaṃ paramaṃ yayau |
stutibhistoṣayāmāsa taṃ praṇamya tatastataḥ || 50-18 ||

p. 485) 	prasannena vipreṇa svavṛttāntasyāviṣkaraṇam

tuṣṭaḥ sa yogādutthāya jagādedaṃ vaco nṛpam |
sālagrāma iti khyātaṃ viṣṇusthānamanuttamam || 50-19 ||

nityaṃ saṃnihitastatra cakrarūpī jagatpatiḥ |
tatra cakrāṅkitaṃ sarvaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ca yat || 50-20 ||

tatra praveśamātreṇa jantavo vītakalmaṣāḥ |
tatra tyaktaśarīrāstu yānti nirvāṇamuttamam || 50-21 ||

tasmin deśe samutpanno brāhmaṇaḥ śiṣṭasaṃmataḥ |
sudarśanaprabhāveṇa sādhitākhilasādhanaḥ || 50-22 ||

gamyate pauṣkaraṃ tīrthaṃ mayādya jagatīpate |
niśi mārgavaśāt prāptaḥ purametat tavarddhimat || 50-23 ||

pihitadvāramālakṣya śamīmūlamiha śritaḥ |

viditavṛttāntena rājñā svapuraṃ prati brāhmaṇasyānayanam

evamuktastato rājā satkṛtyainamaninditam || 50-24 ||

prāveśayat purīṃ ramyāṃ tatrāpyāyatanaṃ hareḥ |
tatroṣitastu tāṃ rātriṃ sarvasatkārapūrvakam || 50-25 ||

aparedyurdvijo gantumārebhe puṣkaraṃ prati |

hitamupadeṣṭuṃ rājaprārthanā

rājā tu kṛtapūrvāhṇakarmāṇaṃ tamabhāṣata || 50-26 ||

p. 486) sādhūnāṃ darśanaṃ puṇyaṃ samastābhīṣṭasādhanam |
iti śrutamato bhāṣe bhagavan kiṃ hitaṃ mama || 50-27 ||

yatheha vindeya sukhaṃ paratrāpyasapatnakam |
tathā prasādaṃ kuru me karuṇārdramanā hyasi || 50-28 ||

tena rājñe sāṅgasaudarśanamahāmantrasyopadeśaḥ

tataḥ prasanno rājānamupasannaṃ (1) mune dvijaḥ |
saudarśanaṃ mahāmantraṃ ṣaḍvarṇaṃ sarvasādhakam || 50-29 ||

satkārapūrvakaṃ tasmai nṛpāyopādiśat tadā |
sāṅgamantraṃ tathā dhyānaṃ sādhanaṃ yantrameva ca || 50-30 ||

astramantrāṃstathā sarvān pravartakanivartakān |
paricchadāni pañcāśadyantritāni mahāmune || 50-31 ||

sarvaṃ tasmai pradāyāsau gantuṃ samupacakrame |

rājñā gurave dakṣiṇayāḥ pradānam

rājā ca paramaprīto dravyajātamupānayat || 50-32 ||

gavāṃ sahasraṃ koṭiṃ ca suvarṇānāṃ tathā mune |

1  atra abhilakṣyeti padamadhyāhṛtya vākyaṃ yojanīyam | upasannapadaṃ ca 
tasmai sa vidvānupasannāya samyakpraśāntacittāya śamānvitāya 
ityādiśrutipratipāditānāmavaśyaṃbhāvināṃ 
śiṣyaguṇāntarāṇāmupalakṣakam | tathāca sa dvijaḥ 
praśrayamṛduvacanādibhirliṅgai rājānaṃ praśāntacittatvādiguṇayuktaṃ 
brahmavidyopadeśayogyamabhilakṣya sāṅgaṃ saudarśanamahāmantraṃ 
tasmai upādiśaditi saṃbandhaḥ

p. 487) ratnapūrṇāni haimāni pātrāṇyanyāni sapta ca || 50-33 ||

tathā bhūyāṃsi vāsāṃsi mahārhāṇi ca bhūpatiḥ |
grāmānaṣṭau ca gurave prādāt paramadhārmikaḥ || 50-34 ||

guruṇā tadagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ svābhimatadeśasya gamanam

na caicchad brāhmaṇastatra dakṣiṇāḥ samupāhṛtāḥ |
kiṃ kāryamebhirnṛpate sarvaṃ saṃkalpite bhavet || 50-35 ||

yatheṣṭaṃ viniyuṅkṣvaitadityuktaḥ prāha bhūpatiḥ |
avaśyaṃ gṛhyatāmetanna cennaivāsmi sādhakaḥ || 50-36 ||

tyajyatāṃ vā yatheṣṭaṃ bho maddattaṃ dattameva hi |
ityukto brāhmaṇo'pyāha rājānamamitaujasam || 50-37 ||

madarthamarthajātaṃ tad brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradīyatām |
ityuktvā prayayau vipro bhūpatiścainamanvayāt || 50-38 ||

nivartitaḥ sa guruṇā brāhmaṇebhyo dhanaṃ dadau |

rājñā dharmato rājyasya praśāsanam

tasmin gate mahārājo viprasātkṛtya taddhanam || 50-39 ||

anvaśāt pṛthivīmetāṃ tridivaṃ maghavāniva |
naktaṃdivaṃ vibhajyaiva dharmamokṣāvirodhinau || 50-40 ||

arthakāmau yathākālamasevata mahīpatiḥ |
rājadharmeṣu cānyeṣu nātyavartiṣṭa kiṃcana || 50-41 ||

p. 488) evaṃ ca mānavaṃ mārgamanutiṣṭhan mahīpatiḥ |
dharmyeṇa pālanenaiva prajā rañjayati sma saḥ || 50-42 ||

vaśyāvaśyapararāṣṭrādibubhutsayā kadācidamātyān prati rājñaḥ 
praśnaḥ

ekadā mantriṇaḥ sarvānāhūyāsthānamaṇḍape |
papraccha pṛthivīpālaḥ ke ca vaśyā utetare || 50-43 ||

deśeṣu bhūmipāleṣu prakṛtiṣvathavā mama |
brūta sarvaṃ hi jānanti bhavantaścāracakṣuṣaḥ || 50-44 ||

mantriṇāṃ prativacanam

iti pratisamādiṣṭāste sarve mantriṇo'vadan |
deva tvayi mahīmetāṃ śāsatyeva paraṃtape || 50-45 ||

pratipakṣāḥ pralīyante tapatyarke yathā tamaḥ |

kṛtsnasyāpi bhūmaṇḍalasya vaśyatvapratipādanam

sādhunā pṛthivī vaśyā saśailāmbudhipattanā || 50-46 ||

divīva deva rājānaḥ sarve dvāri nivāritāḥ |
tava prakṛtivargeṣu kā kathā jagatīpate || 50-47 ||

devādīnāṃ vaśyatvābhāvapratipādanam

na ca devā na gandharvā nāsurā na ca pannagāḥ |
yathā vayaṃ te sevante bhavantaṃ parameśvaram || 50-48 ||

devaḥ pramāṇamiti te vijñāpyārthamupāraman |

p. 489) 	punaramātyān prati rājño devādivijayopāyapraśnaḥ

kīrtimālī ca tacchrutvā devādīnāṃ jigīṣayā || 50-49 ||

vyajṛmbhata tadā sarvān mantriṇaścābravīdidam |
kathametān vijeṣyāmo balino vīryaśālinaḥ || 50-50 ||

vicitrayodhino dṛptā māyābalasamanvitāḥ |
anukūlā viśiṣṭāśca lokāntaramupāśritāḥ || 50-51 ||

yathā bhavanti te vaśyāstathā vadata mā ciram |

amātyānāṃ prativacanam

ityuktāste mahārājamūcuḥ saṃmantrya mantriṇaḥ || 50-52 ||

etānadyaiva yāsyāmo vijeṣyāmaśca saṃyuge |

svapakṣe balasamṛddhinirūpaṇapūrvakamabhiṣeṇanodyogajananam

asmadvarūthinī sarvā caturaṅgabalānvitā || 50-53 ||

merumandarasaṃkāśā gajāḥ paramadurjayāḥ |
ekaikameva paryāptaṃ śatruśainyapramardane || 50-54 ||

evaṃvidhānāṃ nāgānāṃ niyutāni daśābhavan |
mattānāṃ turagāṇāṃ ca pañca koṭyaḥ paraṃtapa || 50-55 ||

tisraḥ koṭyaśca saṃpūrṇāḥ syandanānāṃ tathābhavan |
baddhāpadānamānānāṃ śūrāṇāmanivartinām || 50-56 ||

p. 490) caturvidhapadātīnāmakṣauhiṇyaścaturdaśa |
niśitāstrāṇi naḥ saṃkhye yānyasaṃkhyāni bhūpate || 50-57 ||

ekataḥ sarvametacca ekataḥ kevalo balī |
atirikto bhavān vīryāditi manyāmahe vayam || 50-58 ||

kiṃca divyāni cāstrāṇi labdhavānasi bhūsurāt |
ebhiḥ parivṛtasyaivaṃ kastiṣṭhet saṃmukhe tava || 50-59 ||

saṃvartavartinaḥ śaṃbhorbhūtānīva didhakṣataḥ |
yatra yatra jigīṣā te vartate tatra mā ciram || 50-60 ||

niyuṅkṣvāsmān mahābāho jayaṃ viddhi kare sthitam |

mantrivacanāt prathamaṃ rājño nāgalokābhiṣeṇanam

ityuktaḥ paramaprītaḥ kīrtimālī mahābalaḥ || 50-61 ||

sarvānamātyānāhūya tattatsenā mayāhṛtā |
nāgalokaṃ prayātvadya yudhyatāmadya pannagaiḥ || 50-62 ||

ahaṃ cādyaiva yāsyāmi samūlabalavāhanaḥ |
ityuktvā taiḥ parivṛto jagāma sa mahīpatiḥ || 50-63 ||

garuḍāstraprabhāveṇa tatra rājño vijayaprāptiḥ

tatra taiḥ pannagairyuddhamabhavadromaharṣaṇam |
tato'cireṇa kālena divyāstreṇa garutmataḥ || 50-64 ||

vijayaṃ prāptavāṃstatra tebhyo ratnādikaṃ karam |
gṛhītvā daityanāśāya manaścakre mahāmanāḥ || 50-65 ||

p. 491) 		tathā daityavijayaḥ

tairyuddhamabhavat tīvraṃ cirakālaṃ mahāmune |
nāśayitvā tu tatsainyaṃ vijayaṃ paramaṃ yayau || 50-66 ||

yakṣagandharvādijayapūrvakaṃ rājñaḥ svanagaragamanam

tataśca yakṣagandharvān siddhavidyādharānapi |
nirjitya samare sarvān kiṃkarānakarod balī || 50-67 ||

etān vijitya samare svapuraṃ punarāyayau |

tatra svaparivāreṣu devānāmadarśanādindraṃ prati saṃdeśapreṣaṇam

āsthānasadmanyāsīno rājabhiḥ parivāritaḥ || 50-68 ||

tathā gandharvayakṣaiśca dānavaiḥ pannageśvaraiḥ |
cakravartī nijāsthāne nāmarān dadṛśe tadā || 50-69 ||

tato manojavaṃ nāma gandharvamidamūcivān |
adya tridivamabhyetya vacanānmama vāsavam || 50-70 ||

brūyā airāvataṃ nāgaṃ kuliśaṃ kalpakaṃ tathā |
aṣṭau cāpsarasaḥ kṣipraṃ madarthaṃ visṛjeti vai || 50-71 ||

rājasaṃdeśāt manojavanāmno dūtasyāmarāvatīgamanam

manojavastathetyuktvā śirasā cakravartinaḥ |
mālāmivājñāṃ saṃgṛhya prayayāvamarāvatīm || 50-72 ||

tatrāsthānamaṇḍape vāsavadarśanam

praviśya tat puraṃ ramyamupamānaṃ paraṃ purām |

p. 492) vāsavaṃ vasubhī rudrairādityaiśca samāvṛtam || 50-73 ||

āsthāmaṇḍapāsīnamapsarogaṇasevitam |
viśvāvasumukhaiḥ siddhaiḥ stūyamānaṃ śacīpatim || 50-74 ||

dadarśa sa tadā devaṃ nāṭyadarśanalālasam |
dṛṣṭvā praṇamya tuṣṭāva virate nṛtyakarmaṇi || 50-75 ||

vāsavena dūtasyābhinandanam

dṛṣṭvā prītaḥ surapatirmanojavamavaikṣata |

dūtenendrāya rājasaṃdeśasya nivedanam

vimalīkṛtamātmānaṃ manyamānaḥ sa vīkṣitaiḥ || 50-76 ||

vyajijñapadyathāvṛttaṃ vṛtrahantre'vilambitam |
deva bhūmaṇḍale kaścit kīrtimālīti viśrutaḥ || 50-77 ||

cakravartī surapate devāyaitannyavedayat |

dūtena rājoktasaṃdeśasyānuvādaḥ

vinirjitā mayā lokāḥ sarve tridivamantarā || 50-78 ||

bhavantaṃ draṣṭumicchāmi sāṃprataṃ tridiveśvara |
ārohaṇārthaṃ nāgendramairāvatamavāritam || 50-79 ||

kalpakaṃ kuliśaṃ cāṣṭau yoṣito visṛjeha naḥ |
itthamājñāpitaṃ tena mahārājena gopate || 50-80 ||

devaḥ pramāṇamatreti virarāma manojavaḥ |

p. 493) 	saṃdeśaśravaṇāt kupitasyendrasya tatpratikriyācaraṇam

tato vihasya maghavānāsthāne devasevite || 50-81 ||

prāha gambhīrayā vācā vijñāyaitāṃ viḍambanām |
vajramairāvataṃ nāgaṃ preṣayāmyadya te ubhe || 50-82 ||

āgatya svayamanyāṃstu samāhṛtya prayātvasau |
ityuktvā preṣayāmāsa vajramairāvataṃ gajam || 50-83 ||

savajreṇairāvatena rājasainyasya vināśanam

niṣkramyobhau viviśatuḥ kīrtimālimahāpurīm |
praviśyairāvato nāgo vajramādāya vāhinīm || 50-84 ||

adṛṣṭenaiva rūpeṇa jaghānaināṃ mahāmune |

bhītairjānapadaiḥ rājñe tadvṛttāntasya nivedanam

hanyamāneṣu sainyeṣu gajavājibhaṭātmasu || 50-85 ||

sarve jānapadā bhītā bhūpāyainnyavedayan |
deva sāmodbhavā nāgā nihatā bhuvi śerate || 50-86 ||

tathā hayottamāḥ śūrā bhaṭāḥ paramaśobhanāḥ |
akasmādeva dṛśyante nihatā jagatīpate || 50-87 ||

sainyavināśaśravaṇāt duḥkhitena rājñā purodhasaṃ prati praśnaḥ

etacchrutvā mahārājaḥ śokena mahatā vṛtaḥ |
purodhasaṃ samāhūya vacanaṃ cedamabravīt || 50-88 ||

vāraṇā vājino yodhā hanyante kāraṇādṛte |
p. 494) na rogo dṛśyate caiṣu na tāpaḥ kālakalpitaḥ || 50-89 ||

nābhicāraḥ parakṛto dṛśyate sunirūpitaḥ |
kimetaccintyatāṃ brahman bhramatīva ca me manaḥ || 50-90 ||

purodhasā devakopasyaiva tatkāraṇatvena nivedanam

ityudīritamākarṇya purodhāścāpyacintayat |
cintayitvā jagādaitad devakopādupāgatam || 50-91 ||

iti śaṅke na śokasya vaśamāgāḥ paraṃtapa |

atrāntare manojavasamāgamaḥ

iti cintayatoreva tayorāgānmanojavaḥ || 50-92 ||

praṇataḥ prāñjalistathau mahārājasya pārśvataḥ |

manojavaṃ prati rājñaḥ praśnaḥ

manojava kimānītamuktaratnacatuṣṭayam || 50-93 ||

kimāha vāsavaḥ sarvaṃ kathayādyāvilambitam |

manojavena rājñe nivedanam

ityuktastena sakalaṃ pṛṣṭamasmai nyavedayat || 50-94 ||

rājavākyasya kathanamato maghavataḥ smayaḥ |
vajrairāvatayostena preṣaṇaṃ tadvacastataḥ || 50-95 ||

p. 495) svasainyavināśamairāvatakṛtaṃ jñātavatā rājñā 
vāraṇāstreṇairāvatasya

evaṃ tenerito rājā niścityairāvatakriyām |
purodhasā pratīkāraṃ tasya saṃmantrya tatkṣaṇāt || 50-96 ||

vyasṛjadvāraṇākhyāstraṃ divyaṃ vāraṇavāraṇam |
tadastraṃ tu gajaṃ prāptaṃ niśceṣṭaḥ so'bhavat tadā || 50-97 ||

tacchrutavatā vāsavena yuddhārthaṃ devasainyasya preṣaṇam

stabdhamairāvataṃ vajraṃ tenāstreṇa mahātmanā |
vyajijñapurmaghavate surāścairāvatānugāḥ || 50-98 ||

tacchrutvā paramakruddho maghavān samanahyata |
saṃnaddhamindramālakṣya surāścukṣubhire tadā || 50-99 ||

preṣitāstena te sarve sainikāstatpuraṃ yayuḥ |
pralayojjṛmbhitāmbhodhikalpaṃ sainyaṃ śatakratoḥ || 50-100 ||

tato devamānuṣasenayorghorayuddhapravṛttiḥ

vilokya tvarito rājā sasainyo niryayau purāt |
tataḥ pravavṛte yuddhaṃ devamānuṣasenayoḥ || 50-101 ||

pravṛttayuddhavarṇanam

adṛṣṭacaramāścaryaṃ hāhākāri bhayāvaham |
divyadundubhinirghoṣaiḥ śaṅkhabherīravairapi || 50-102 || 

kṣvelitaistalaśabdaiśca vihāyaḥ samapūryata |
nāgā yuyudhire nāgaiḥ pattibhiḥ pattayastathā || 50-103 ||

p. 496) syandanaiḥ syandanāścāśvairaśvāścaiva parasparam |
nārācaiḥ parighaiḥ śūlaiḥ paṭṭasairmudgarairapi || 50-104 ||

kārmukaiḥ śaktibhiḥ khaḍgaiḥ prāsaiḥ kuntaiḥ paraśvadhaiḥ |
evaṃ nānāvidhaiḥ śastrairnijaghnuste parasparam || 50-105 ||

kecinnikṛttaśirasaḥ kecicchinnorasaḥ paraiḥ |
kecidutkṛttaśastrāstu viddhavakṣāṃsi kāni ca || 50-106 ||

chinnahastā gajāḥ kecit khaḍganirbhinnamastakāḥ |
bhallairnirbhinnahṛdayā dṛśyante turagāstathā || 50-107 ||

pravṛttā raktasarito hastisyandanavājinām (1) |
nipatacchastrasaṃbhinnā dṛśyante raṇabhūmayaḥ || 50-108 ||

tatra jigīṣuṇendreṇa svasenādhipatīnāṃ pracodanam

evaṃ pravṛtte saṃgrāme jayārthī pākaśāsanāḥ |
devasenāpatīn sarvāṃścodayāmāsa satvaraḥ || 50-109 ||

devasenāpatīnāṃ tātkālikajayaprāptiḥ

tena pravartitā devā vidrāvya nṛpaterbalam |
vijayaṃ lebhire sadyastataḥ kruddho'bhavannṛpaḥ || 50-110 ||
